# DM notes: Ash on Ostrava (hidden)

**Never read any of this out to the player.** It holds secrets, nemeses and pre-rolled results; the player learns them only in play.

**How to read this file:** read down to the **STOP** line on every resume (it's short, and it includes the index of triggers). Below it are the entries: open one only when its trigger comes up. When a thread closes, move it to `archive.md`.

---

## How the campaign runs (always)

- **It runs until Tamsin dies** (the player's rule, session 8; never bring it up at the table). No planned ending. If he dies it's final: the book closes as his memorial and the player starts a new character.
- **The longer he lives, the harder it gets**, through more enemies sized to the job and to the Light (most fights 10-30; a kill team sent after the Light is about its size, 6-12; a ship or compound at the top end; small fights still happen), smarter tactics (flanking, ambushes, focusing the wounded, falling back to better ground) and nastier ground. **Never** moved DCs, inflated stats or per-book roll bonuses, and never a rigged death: the dice stand both ways. Each enemy rolls its own STR and DEX. The Light keeps pace through gear.
- **Enemies use their roles together, the way the player would:** a bomb zealot under smoke and covering fire, snipers on high ground, spotters marking for them. Type still shows: cultists are reckless and sometimes just charge; pirates and traitors are cold and careful.
- The player is a collector: put interesting things in the world; invented gear is fine if it fits. In the field, carry what's plausible.
- The player's d20s run hot (about 14.5 average in sessions 4-5). The dice stand; shot chains were the player's own answer.

## Live now: the Torvane's Mart mission (from day 33)

- **The rising (the player's twist, accepted):** when the Light drops out of the warp, **Governor Halden Voke is broadcasting an open distress call.** Thask's gardeners and the dock gangs he bought are trying to take the asteroid, with heavy casualties. Planned detail for the reveal: Low Quay and the customs house have fallen, the militia is broken, Voke holds **the Spire** (north pole) with his guard. Layout to show: the Spire at the north pole, Low Quay and berth 31 at the south end, the main dock ring, the vox mast, the cannery near berth 44.
  - Why now: Thask moved early because the Light gutted his Ustrennos line (the circle, Hob, Charter 7).
  - The Governor asking for help overrides Kesh's "no open war"; Thask is still wanted alive; the Bloom is on Tamsin's judgment.
  - **Brutal**, run as several fights of 10-30: Valkyrie drop, objective, extract, repeat.
- **Fight 1 done: the Spire pad (~1106-1107 day 33).** 14 raiders (Hookmen and Tallow Boys; `roll_enemy.py raider 14 poor`; group d20 2: no rare gear in use). The group broke at 8 Down; break d20 7: **the rest surrendered.**
  - **Dead (d6 1-3):** the Ogryn (#3; Corvane's power sword), the gunslinger (#5; Varga), the ripper gunner (#8; Kell), the rogue psyker (#7; Tamsin's Stinger burst, two crits, hit again while Down), #12 pipe rifle (Varga), #14 pump shotgun (the Governor's guards).
  - **Alive but Down (d6 4-6):** #1 (junk shotgun; near roof, behind the water tank), #6 (junk shotgun + stolen shock baton; near roof, by the vents).
  - **Surrendered:** far roof #2 (Rattler SMG; Badly hurt), #4 (autopistol; Hurt), #9 (nailer; Fine); on the main stair facing Dace and Corvane: #10 (knife, autopistol; Hurt), #11 (shank, Quicksilver; Badly hurt), #13 (shank, Hammerfall auto-revolver; Badly hurt).
  - Loot not yet rolled (looting d20 natural 20 = stashed rare); coin on bodies per codex Part 8, Loot.
  - **~1245: the gardeners cut the Spire's air feed (d20 3). Reserve tanks: 10 hours, to ~2245.** The customs strongroom still holds (~1250, d20 13). **~1250: the Hookmen gunship lifted (d20 9) and is heading for the mast (d4 2)**; **The gunship (rolled): a scav-rigged cargo lifter, nose heavy stubber + a 4-tube rocket pod (d6 4), the pilot + 4 Hookmen in the back (d4 4).** Vehicle damage (DM default): a hit drops it one step (Fine, Hurt, Badly hurt, Down = crashes), a solid hit two, a crit downs it. Brenner's read 16+8 = 24 (all of that, and its engine is running rough). Teo's turret: natural 1, then 15 vs 17: two misses; it's jinking. **Anti-air beat (~1252):** orders Morcant (light it up: 2+4 = 6, miss) and Isk (the pilot, target 22: 3+9 = 12, miss); everyone else open fire; Tamsin takes Vigil (heat 0, cleared after the mast fight) and puts three into the rocket pod (target 19; a hit wrecks the pod, a solid hit or crit cooks the rockets off: the lifter drops two steps). Hull target 17. Pilot rulings: a hit on him = Hurt and the lifter lurches (its attacks -2 this beat); a solid hit or crit = he goes Down and the lifter crashes. Kell is at the Spire: no leader +1. The gunship's attack this beat: rockets at the Valkyrie on the deck (ten prisoners and Mira aboard). **Tamsin: 3+11 = 14 miss; 17+11-1 = 27 CRIT; 19+11+1 = 31 CRIT (Vigil heat 2, cleared when it ended): the rockets cooked off in the pod and the lifter blew apart before it could fire.** **It fell onto an occupied tenement block on the dock ring below the east face (d6 5; d20 3): the building is burning, civilians hurt.** Crew: **the pilot survived (5)**, two Hookmen survived (6, 6), two dead (1, 3); the three are crawling out of the wreck. ~1253. **Isk's look (~1255, 19+3 = 22):** the pilot (burned arm) and the two Hookmen (one dragging a leg) are **trapped on the burning roof** (d20 20), fire between them and the stairwell; the fire is held to the tenement's top two floors for now (16); 7 civilians out in the street and 4 down (2 moving, 2 still); **a woman, two children and an old man trapped at a third-floor east window**, the stairwell full of smoke. No militia, no fire crews; the looters ran.
  - **~1344: the vox mast is unguarded** (Tamsin sent Dorrow's militia back to the Spire). If the Hookmen regroup, it can be lost again; roll if it matters.
  - **The objectives clock:** ~1118 the vox mast is still standing but contested (d20 6: one bad roll from falling); lower gates holding (Moll). **The scrubber hall fell ~1000 to the gardeners themselves** (Voke's last runner; d20 2). They haven't cut the Spire's air yet; if they do, the Spire's reserve tanks last **10 hours** (d6+6 = 10); the rest of the rock has less. **What the gardeners want with the air plant is not set**: let it follow the story (it could tie to the jars and 'the garden opens'). Customs-house strongroom: last heard ~1000. **Teo's second sweep (~1128, rolled):** the mast: **26 Hookmen** attacking up the ridge road vs **14 militia** on the platform (roll `raider 26 poor` when the Light engages; whether Dagan Crale is there: roll on arrival); the customs house: **~25, gardeners and Tallow Boys mixed** above ground (roll cultist Good + raider poor groups when engaged); berth 31: heavy heat, no ship with warm engines (3); the tug dock: quiet, a few heat signatures (6); the dock ring: gangs scattered and looting (9); the scrubber hall: lots of heat under the ring plating, can't be counted from orbit, the plant still running.
  - The Governor's guards: 3 of 7 left. Mira still on the pad (asked whether to lift; no answer yet).
- **Fight 2, no shots fired: a looting crew on the way to the mast (~1145 day 33). They surrendered** when Tamsin stepped out with Vigil and the Light poured round the corner (reaction d20 6 - 4 = 2). All four disarmed and held in the street. Route encounter d20 14 (small group), d4+2 = 4, looting a chandler's shop on the dock ring's west side; Tallow Boys. `roll_enemy.py raider 4 poor`: basic group, group d20 4 (no rare, better-armed 0). Neglected jams 1-3 (nat 1 seizes); Dirty jams on a nat 1.
  - #1 grunt, melee: scrap throwing knives (4), flak armour set, lho-sticks; STR +1 DEX +2 (inside the shop).
  - #2 FLAMER: hand flamer + 1 canister, flak vest; STR +3 DEX +2; Neglected (outside, by the door). Tank can burst on a solid hit (d6: 1-4 he goes up, 5-6 the gun).
  - #3 VOX/AUSPEX: auspex + lascarbine, leathers; STR +2 DEX +2; Dirty (outside). **The group can't be ambushed while he's on the team**; spotted the Light through the wall.
  - #4 grunt, ranged: nailer (scrap scattergun), combat knife, flak vest, frag; STR +3 DEX +2; Dirty (inside the shop).
  - Spotting: Corvane 2+4 = 6 vs the crew 4+1 = 5: the Light saw them first, but the auspex gives them warning (no ambush).
- **Group 3 (ahead, ~1152 day 33):** route d20 13: **3 Tallow Boys dragging 4 civilians** (d3+1 = 4), ~200 m up the street toward the ridge, right side. Rolled `raider 3 poor` (basic group, group d20 7: no rare, better-armed 0):

  - **Survival (d6, 1-3 dead):** Knife crank man 5 alive; Bottle crank man 1 dead; Sawn-off tender 2 dead; Chainsword tender 1 dead; Breacher paralysed (alive by the needle ruling); Catwalk gunner 6 alive; Scattergun tender 3 dead; Revolver hauler 1 dead; Machete hauler 4 alive. Flamer and Singer dead. **Alive: Knife crank man, Breacher, Catwalk gunner, Machete hauler** (no prisoners: left where they fell unless Tamsin says otherwise). The 12 in the beds are alive, still buried.
  - **~1404:** orders Morcant ('open that bolt') and Brenner ('burn it open if he can't'). **Morcant 6+6 = 12 vs 12: the bolt ground open.** Sallow (the medic, at the bars) dragged out the two not breathing and worked both: **dock worker 11+7 = 18, girl 15+5 = 20 (split -2): BOTH REVIVED.** All four from the cage alive (two coughing up sludge, weak). Tamsin: 'gather everything up for extract'; inside loot rolled (looting d20 10, nothing rare), heretic stuff left per his standing order; carried up to the barricade pile for Mira (due ~1404).
  - **~1405:** Tamsin took Varga, Corvane, Tavi and Hollis to find the valve; everyone else loads the Valkyrie (Mira landed by the gate barricade) and meets up after; comms if trouble. **The north valve house (rolled): empty inside (d20 6); the steel door is locked (d6 3; no key turned up in the loot; where one is, if anywhere, is rolled if they search); the override wheel inside is just shut (d6 5): open it and the Spire breathes.** Through the door's vision slit: a big red wheel on the main pipe, a gauge needle on zero. #6 and #9 still breathing at the sandbags; #2 dead behind them; the assassin dead under the grating. The 16 captives (4 from the cage, 12 in the beds) still in the hall.
  - **~1406:** searched the valve house bodies quietly for the key: **d6 3: on Lasgun gardener #9** (breathing, out cold; didn't stir, d20 18): a heavy iron key on a cord round his neck under his flak, brass tag stamped 'N. VALVE'. Varga found it. Nothing else new on the bodies (already looted).
  - **~1407:** 'Open it' (low): Varga unlocked the door. Inside: one room, empty (as rolled); the Spire main pipe with the red override wheel shut (just shut, nothing rigged), gauge on zero; wilted flower garlands tied round the wheel's spokes (an offering, harmless); a dead wall vox panel.
  - **~1408:** Tamsin's Perception 14+6 = 20 (no Kell): nothing rigged, nobody hidden; fresh pry-bar gouges on the wheel's rim (forced shut fast), a crowbar behind the pipe; the flowers are tied with twine. **The Spire line's air (rolled d20 10, 1-5 tainted): clean**; a hairline leak at the flange smells cold and metallic, not sweet.
  - **~1409: THE SPIRE'S AIR IS BACK ON.** Tamsin, Varga, Corvane, Tavi and Hollis heaved the override wheel open (five on a stiff wheel and the crowbar; no roll needed). The gauge climbed to the green; the main thundered. Mira relayed the Spire (Captain Moll) on her band: air coming through the Spire's vents, people cheering in the halls. **Objective done: the Spire breathes** (it had ~8.5 h of reserve left). The scrubber hall is the Light's; the 16 captives are still in it.
  - **~1410:** Tamsin on the vox: everything loaded? (yes) 'Take off, then come back for prisoners and civs.' **Mira lifted with the hall's loot for the *Light* (~1410; back ~1430).** Nobody rode with it. The loading party (Kell, Sallow, Dace, Morcant, Tey, Sorel, Isk, Brenner, Nessa) walking over to the valve house as ordered. **Prisoners now wanted (the no-prisoners order lifted):** 8 gardeners breathing: outside Staff woman (psyker, Down), Club man #4 (Down), Autogun gardener #6, Lasgun gardener #9 (Down, out cold); inside Knife crank man, Catwalk gunner, Machete hauler (Down), Shield gardener (breacher, paralysed). Civs: 16 (4 from the cage, weak; 12 buried in the beds, not dug out yet). **The Valkyrie takes ~12 a run: 24 needs two runs**; Mira asked which first.
  - **~1411:** Tamsin: 'take the civs first'; 'finish the cultists, they are of no use to us.' **The Light executed the 8 breathing gardeners** (Staff woman (psyker), Club man, Autogun gardener, Lasgun gardener outside; Knife crank man, Catwalk gunner, Machete hauler, Shield gardener inside): one las round each, Kell's call. No prisoners from the scrubber hall. **The Light dug the 12 out of the beds** while Mira's away (implied by 'civs first'). **Their state (d20 16, 16-20 better than it looks): the fungus is surface rot, it scrapes off; weak, starved, dehydrated, but all twelve will live.** Mira's runs: **run 1 (~1430): 12 civs to the Spire's medicae (the 4 from the cage + 8 from the beds); run 2: the last 4.**
- **~1434: Tamsin switches to Thask now, by Nessa's plan** (Thask thinks the Light are retaking ground). **Nessa's brief (now set):**
  - **No docker coats** were ever bought; it doesn't matter now (the Quay is in open riot, nobody's checking).
  - **Getting there:** not through customs. Mira flies low round the rock's outer skin (away from customs) to the **tug pens** at the south tip; tugs moor against the outer hull, and a pilots' lock leads into the tug dock (Teo's sweep: quiet, a handful of heat).
  - **The Gut:** from the tug dock, the tug crews' maintenance crawl runs under the Quay's deck plating to the bonded warehouses. Berth 31's branch ends at **the tallyman's hatch**, a floor hatch in the lower hold where untaxed cargo came up past customs. Thask had it cut; Nessa used it for years running his guns. Tight: crouching, single file; heavy carapace fits but is slow and loud. Nessa's advice: **8 at most** through the Gut, the rest with Mira as the loud backup over the roof. If the hatch has been welded, Tavi cuts it.
  - **Inside:** the lower hold (bonded cargo, crates, a cargo lift); **the counting room** off the hold (Ilsabet and the ledger); the main stair up through the floors; **the top floor: the Bloom's room, no windows, one door, Thask sleeps across it.** ~20 gardeners in the warehouse and the floors above (old count).
  - **Exit:** back down the Gut to the tug dock, or up to the roof for Mira if it goes loud.
  - **Prizes:** Thask alive (Kesh); the Bloom on Tamsin's judgment; Ilsabet and the ledger.
  - Not set yet (roll on arrival): whether the hatch is guarded or welded, where Thask, the Bloom and Ilsabet are right now.
- **~1436: the Gut team:** Tamsin, Varga, Brenner, Nessa, Tavi, Corvane, Tey, Sallow. **Backup with Mira:** Kell, Dace, Morcant, Isk, Hollis, Sorel. Mira back ~1450, took the last four civilians to the Spire, back ~1510; lifted both teams. **Low run round the outer skin (d20 12+6 = 18 vs 14): unseen.** ~1520: the Gut team dropped at the tug pens' pilots' lock; Mira holds off the south tip, out of sight, with the backup six.
- **The tug dock (rolled ~1520):** Thask guards his back way. **The Moebian 6th** (cultist special team, Nurgle; 2d20 37: +5 STR/DEX levels, +1 other rolls, armour one step better, +3 better-armed; once a Guard regiment, gas masks fused to faces, sores, flies; they hold and grind, gas first). 5 of them, **on watch and alert** (d20 12): #1 grunt ranged: rusted Kantrael lasgun + chainsword, **carapace** (chest plate stepped up), Frenzon, found: stolen krak; STR+3 DEX+3; Neglected. **#2 BRUTE (mutant):** chained engine-block flail, carapace (stepped up), found: cult-forged blade; STR+3; +2 health stages (brute). #3 grunt melee: 4 scrap throwing knives, carapace chest (flak stepped up); STR+2 DEX+2. **#4 BEAST HANDLER + 4 warp-hounds** (daemon-touched; 1 health state each; wild if the handler dies), whip, carapace chest (stepped up), frag. #5 grunt ranged: **braced autogun ('Graia')** + chain knife, carapace (stepped up), black candles and chalk; STR+2 DEX+2; Dirty. **Positions (set ~1521):** the tug dock is a ~50 m bay, tug lock collars along the outer wall (the Light's lock is the last, at the near end), fuel drums and crates down the middle, the crane gantry at the back with the Gut's mouth behind it. **#3 knife thrower** pacing ~10 m from the lock doors; **#1 lasgun** by the fuel drums mid-dock (~25 m); **#5 braced autogun** set up on the gantry's base (~40 m), covering the whole dock; **#2 brute** sitting on a crate by the Gut's mouth (~45 m); **#4 handler + 4 hounds** chained by the Gut's mouth (~45 m).
  - **~1521:** Brenner's auspex 6+6 = 12 vs 12: five warm bodies, one close (~10 m), one mid-dock, three at the back by the gantry; four cold, flickering blips at the back that won't hold a lock (the hounds).
  - **~1522:** Tamsin's Perception through a scraped corner of the porthole 19+6 = 25: sees all five and the hounds, the braced autogun covering the lock doors, the tug fuel drums (promethium) next to the lasgun man. Nobody inside has looked at the lock.
- **THE TWIST (set by the player, session 10, ~1523): the Moebian 6th have been behind the whole thing.** A traitor Guard regiment rotted into a Nurgle cult; they still move and plan like a real regiment, infuse themselves with the rot, and field **swarms of poxwalkers**. They are the mind and the muscle behind the Gardener's network: the jars grown in Gallowmere's silos ('the garden-world'), the plague, Thask's gardeners, the Bloom and 'the garden opens' at the Harvest Lamps, and the Mart rising's real plan (air plant, vox mast, customs house: a regiment's objectives). Thask is their broker and front. **Still open (let it follow the story):** who commands the 6th and where (Gallowmere?); whether 'the Gardener' is Thask or one of theirs; how far back it goes (Season 1's Seed of Nurgle?); how many of them are on the Mart. Tamsin knows them by reputation (the player's lore: whoever fights them doesn't come out well; poxwalker swarms; rot-infused; regimental).
- **Fight: the tug dock (~1524).** Tamsin: 'no more prisoners, no more quiet.' Not surprised (alert), but the Light act first. No Kell: no +1. Orders: Varga ('the hound guy') +2; **'Hollis, lob some' void: Hollis is on the Valkyrie**, so Nessa's order ('the one close') takes the second +2.

Tey 4/6/7 (+7): 11, 11, 12, all miss on the Gantry gunner. Tavi 5+5 = 10: miss on the gunner. **Brenner 11+4 = 15: a hound dead. Sallow natural 20: a hound dead. Corvane's revolver 16+6 = 22 and 18+6+2-1 = 25: two hounds dead (all four dead)**; his third 12, miss.

Hound handler ducked behind a gantry leg with his frag out.

Tey 4+9 = 13: Flail brute Grazed (first extra stage); natural 1; 4+9-2 = 11: miss.** **Tamsin's Vigil (into the handler until he drops, then the gunner): 11+11 = 22 vs 17 (gantry leg cover): Hound handler Badly hurt; 16+11+2-1 = 28: Down, and he dropped his live frag; 20 CRIT swapped to the Gantry gunner: Down.** Vigil heat 2. **The frag burst at the handler's feet: the handler (Down) dead; the gunner within it (d6 1), hit again while Down: dead.** Tavi 10, Brenner 11, Sallow natural 1: missed the brute. Corvane drew his power sword and held the line; Nessa moved up behind the crates (brute out of needle range). **Break check (4 of 5 down): Flail brute d20 8: fights on.** He walked on ~10 m: **~25 m from the Light.**

Applied properly from here; earlier results stand.

RELIEF FROM THE HOLD'** (d6 6: **the relief comes up the Gut from berth 31's hold at ~2000**). Heretic stuff left (the brute's cult blade, the gunner's black candles and chalk).

Options: Brenner's Navy cutting torch (quiet-ish, slow, glow and hot-metal smell), Tavi's cutting charge (fast, loud).
- **Above the hatch (rolled):** one gardener sitting on a crate beside it (d20 20, d4 1), talking with another voice farther off in the hold (d20 20). Overheard: Miss Ilsabet is burning the books on the Old Man's orders ('if the Light gets in, there's nothing left to read'); the Old Man hasn't left the girl's door since noon.

The blast slammed the sitter off his crate: **Hurt.** Loud: the whole building knows (Thask upstairs too). Tavi's kraks: 3 + 1 stolen - 1 = 3.

Lift gunner's stub automatic at Tamsin: 12+2 = 14 hit, 16+2+2-1 = 19 hit (**both stopped by the refractor: 3 this beat, the limit**), 11 miss. **Crate gunner's autogun: natural 3: JAMMED (Neglected).**

  - **Beat 1:** **Tamsin's impact frag 14+7 = 21 vs 15 (solid: through carapace), ~2 m behind the barricade: 13 caught (all but the psyker), no dive: all Hurt.** **Corvane's frag 8+8 = 16 (not solid: carapace shrugs it): dives vs 15: #1 18, #4 19, #14 19 clear; carapace shrugged #7, #5, #13 (wear 1 each); Badly hurt: #12 berserker, #2, #6, #8, #10, #11, #3 icon bearer.** **Tavi's krak 19+7 = 26: blew the wardrobe out of the barricade's middle (a hole), and #7 behind it: Badly hurt.** Nobody Down.

The Light unhurt; Tamsin's heavy front wear 1.

Orders: Tavi blow the door; Mira drop the team on the roof (assumed: she picks team B up at the pilots' lock, on the roof in ~5 min, ~1559); Varga drag Thask down the stairs out of the way. The Light circled well back from the door, guns up. Thask, dragged off: 'Don't hurt her! She's never even seen the sky!' **Tavi's breaching charge 5+7 = 12 vs 13: messy**: the door blew in off its hinges and splinters sprayed the room; **the girl was on the far side (d20 9): untouched.**
- **THE BLOOM (rolled d6 2 on a DM table: 1-2 a girl, 3-4 a girl who is something more, 5-6 not a girl): SHE IS A GIRL. Just a girl.** **Her name: Maren** (told to Sallow in the medicae; rolled day 35, d10 1). About to turn twelve (the Harvest Lamps, ~day 55). Pale as paper (never seen the sun), thin, long dark hair, a white nightdress; healthy, bright-eyed, **never sick a day**. The room: windowless, painted floor to ceiling with a garden mural and a blue sky; potted flowers under warm lamps everywhere; a narrow bed, books, a few old toys, a little writing desk. **The cleanest room on the Mart.** She has only ever seen Thask ('Father'), Ilsabet, and masked gardeners at the door. Thask's warning was a bluff, or love. **Now set (the twist):** she's the 6th's perfect host; her blood carries the rot without it touching her: the seed stock behind the jars and 'the garden opens'. What exactly happens at the Lamps is still open. Kesh's leave: kill her only if she's a thing, not a girl, or if taking her risks the Light.
  - **~1555:** Tamsin walked up to her and looked her over: **Perception 8+6 = 14 vs 12:** no weapon, nothing hidden, no wards or tricks in the room, no sores or marks of rot anywhere on her; **but the inside of both elbows is a mass of old pinpricks and fresh needle marks** (her blood is drawn regularly: the seed stock). She's terrified, asks for her father again.
  - **~1556:** Sallow in with the girl; the rest searched Thask's quarters and the top floor. **Loot (looting d20 6, nothing rare): 188 Thrones** (icon bearer 21, witch 27, berserker 26, grunts 114). Heretic stuff left. **Thask's quarters, evidence (d20 15, good; now set):** (1) **a bundle of letters tied in green ribbon, sealed in green wax with a chalk gate and a '6'**, in a cramped soldier's hand, signed only with the gate; the latest: 'The Lamps are near. Keep her clean. The garden-world is ready to receive her. Ten a head for the beds; we will need them all.' (2) **Thask's black notebook:** years of dated entries, 'drawn: 2 vials', 'sent with the Tender', the amounts growing, ties the girl's blood to the jars and the *Hesk Tender*. **His strongbox is NOT in his quarters (d20 13); it's elsewhere in the building (most likely the counting room), d10 5: 10,000 Thrones if found.** The hold's six bodies not looted yet.
  - **Thask's Will (set day 37 ~0824, hidden; house rule 8): starts 85%** (a believer for decades, 'speaks for the gardeners'; surrendered only to keep the girl safe); **breaking point 15%** (a fanatic: almost never breaks). What gets in with him: the girl (her health, her 'cleanness', who has touched her), Grandfather mocked or proven absent, the 6th and Wolfer abandoning him. What doesn't: threats to himself (he's been 'dying for twenty years'). **~0826: the girl into the Emperor's light, another family; the room gone; 'I know' (the girl: gets in): Intimidation 3+6 = 9, -1%: Will 84%.** **~0827: her purity as the Emperor's choice, her killing Wolfer; 'probably not even your daughter' (his faith's claim on her: gets in): Intimidation 11+6 = 17, -6%: Will 78%.**
  - **MAREN IS NOT THASK'S BLOOD (set day 37 ~0827, hidden roll d6 1; table: 1-2 not his, found; 3-4 his by blood, a late child; 5-6 his wife's, not his):** about eleven years ago a quarantined hauler was opened at the Low Quay with everyone aboard dead of the rot, except one infant, untouched among them. Thask took her as Grandfather's sign and raised her as his own; he named her the Bloom; Ilsabet (then ~19) named her Maren. **Ilsabet knows. Maren doesn't.** Tamsin's jab was true; Thask didn't deny it. **~0828: Ilsabet said it aloud (d6 2, her own choice, to cut her sister loose from him): 'He didn't make her. He found her.' and 'I named her. Not you.' She hasn't said where or how (the hauler) yet.**
  - **WHAT THE BLOOM'S BLOOD IS FOR (set day 37 ~0822; hidden roll d6 5; table: 1-2 her immunity: the 6th culture a 'quiet bloom' in it, a strain that spreads while the host stays well; 3-4 faith only: her 'clean' blood is a sacrament mixed into the jars, the 6th want her as the garden's living saint; 5-6 BOTH): BOTH.** She is just a girl, naturally untouched by the rot; the 6th and Thask believe Grandfather made her so, and her blood is the garden's sacrament, a few drops in every jar's culture. **And it works, without any warp in it:** cultured in her blood, the rot learns to sleep: the jars' strain spreads for weeks while the carriers stay well, then blooms all at once (the quiet bloom). At twelve, at the Lamps, she was to go to the garden-world to be the living source for the silos, and its saint. Nobody aboard has said this yet.
  - **Ilsabet's Will (set day 37 ~0815, hidden; house rule 8): starts 75%** (four days caged, her sister kept from her; the bookkeeper, not a zealot, but her father's daughter); **breaking point 35%** (she'd break for her sister). **Knows the name Wolfer (d6 1): she's read it** (the green-ribbon letters came through her hands to be filed). **Why she burned the newest books (hidden roll d6 5; table: 1-2 to protect the network, 3-4 to protect herself, 5-6 to protect her sister): TO PROTECT MAREN** (the last years held the pages naming the Bloom as the 6th's prize, the blood dates and the Lamps passage; she believed the Inquisition would read them, judge her sister a cult asset and burn her). **~0815: Intimidation 16+6 = 22, -16%: Will 59%.** **~0818 (new rule: 1-10% a check): 'You want something from me? For free?' (the sister's fate as a bargaining chip: gets in) Intimidation 3+6 = 9, -2%: Will 57%.** **~0820: the daybook she didn't burn (new proof: gets in): Intimidation 10+6 = 16, -8%: Will 49%.** She knows the *Hesk Tender*'s real hull name (from the books; not yet given, not yet set).
  - **Maren's Will (set day 37 ~0800, hidden; house rule 8): starts 65%** (four days of kindness, the tour, the left-seat promise; but she loves her father and keeps his rules); **breaking point 40%** (a child, not a believer: what she guards is Father's rules, not faith). **Free (no check): her room, the flowers, the books, Father across the door, Ilsabet's visits, the masked men at the door, the needles ('to keep me well').** **Guarded (Father's rule: never talk about the garden to outside people):** that 'the garden opens' when she's twelve, at the Lamps; that Father said a ship would come at the Lamps to take her to the real garden, with a real sky. **Will 64%** after the first push (~0800: Persuasion 7+2 = 9, -1%). **~0805, the truth about Thask: Persuasion natural 20 +2 = 22, 18% doubled = -36%: Will 28%, BELOW the breaking point (40%): she breaks** (answers truthfully what she knows; the guarded garden secret given unprompted).
  - The girl, asked who her father is: Orlen Thask. 'Father keeps me safe. He says the air outside makes people sick. Everyone except me.' Ilsabet is her sister.
- **~1557:** Tamsin, angrier than the Light have ever seen him, on the vox: 'Everyone on this place now, we are sweeping this place clean. That's an order.' To Varga: 'Load up the loot and the prisoners. I'll be back.' (Assumed: Varga takes Thask up to the roof; Sallow carries the girl to the Valkyrie.) **Mira lands team B on berth 31's roof ~1600**; Kell's six come down to sweep the building. Tamsin went down alone to the counting room; **Tey followed (his standing place at Tamsin's side)**.
- **The counting room (rolled ~1558):** **door locked** (d6 3; frosted glass); **Ilsabet alone** (d20 2), **unarmed** (d20 16). **Thask's strongbox is in here** (d20 15): 10,000 Thrones. The ledger fire: ~15 of ~40 minutes burned (roughly a third gone; ash by ~1623 if she keeps feeding it). Ilsabet (now set): about thirty, spectacles, hair pinned up, ink-stained fingers, sleeves rolled, soot on her face; feeding ledgers into an iron stove one by one.

Roughly a third of the books burned.

(Where they came from, now set: the 6th's 'beds' at the customs house: the people bought at ten a head.) **The Hookmen hit the Spire's lower gates** (d6 5): **~12** (2d10+5). **The Tallow Boys under Marl Tegg go for Old Wick in the Spire's cells** (d6 5): **~8** (d6+5), alongside the Hookmen (a joint push). Moll's ~22 guards hold the gates for now. **~0200 day 34: Moll vox-calls the Light for help; Teo's augur shows the shamblers on the ring.** The decision is Tamsin's (outcomes not rolled until he acts or doesn't).

(The Spire left to Moll.) **Hidden pre-roll: the Spire's gates over the next hour d20 12: held, with losses** (reveal by vox when it comes up). **Mira's dash (4+4 = 8): the Light arrive as the swarm reaches the hall's gate.** **The swarm: ~26 poxwalkers** (bestiary DM default: slow, one health state, disgustingly resilient on a 6, mindless, bites carry the rot). **One herder of the 6th behind them** (d20 20, d2 1): braced autogun 'Graia' (Neglected), chain knife, carapace (stepped up from flak), Frenzon; STR+2 DEX+2, +1 other rolls; ~40 m behind the swarm on the ring, goading them with a bell on a pole. Tag in play: **Bell-man (grunt)**. Kill him and the swarm loses its march (turns on the nearest living).
- **Fight: the scrubber hall, night (~0212 day 34).** 'Drop us behind them. Get ready, team, we're going in hot.' **Mira's hot landing 11+4 = 15: clean**, ~25 m behind the Bell-man on the open ring; she lifts off to circle. **The Bell-man (d6 4) turned the swarm on the Light**: the bell's rhythm changed and **23 poxwalkers turned back from the gate ramp toward the Light (~65 m off, 5 m a beat)**; **3 were already over the barricade and wandered down the ramp into the hall** (loose inside). **His braced autogun at the ramp: natural 2 on the first shot: JAMMED (Neglected).** He's backing toward the swarm to keep it between himself and the Light. No Kell +1 issue: Kell is here (+1 applies).

Tey 7+8 = 15 one down (not solid), 12+8+2-1 = 21 one, 14+8+2-1 = 23 one.** Resilience on the 3 non-solid hits (d6 2, 3, 3): none get up. **12 poxwalkers down.** **The Bell-man is Down: the swarm lost its march** and comes on loose at the nearest living things, the Light.

- **The customs house (rolled ~0225).** Layout (Voke's plans; the Light know it): a two-storey rockcrete gatehouse straddling the road from the dock ring to the Low Quay; a vehicle arch through its middle, its heavy shutter half down; offices above with firing slits; a flat roof with a sandbag nest and **a stolen stab-light sweeping the approach**; **razor wire across the road ~30 m out** (a gap in the middle for their own); sandbag nests either side of the arch; **the yard behind (south) holds the 6th's 'beds'** and **~4 new poxwalkers**; **the strongroom under it** (Voke's ~20, vox channel and codes on the ship's files). **Alert** (they heard the hall fight and sent 'Thorn').
  - **The 6th (3 + a sergeant; Moebian bonuses: +5 STR/DEX levels, +1 other rolls, armour one step better):** **SERGEANT (leader):** desecrated chainsword (-1 to parry with it) + autopistol, carapace; at the arch. **Trooper:** scrap drum autogun + Rashad axe, carapace chest (stepped up), stimm; on the roof nest. **Trooper:** Cadian machete + sawn-off, carapace chest, choke gas; with the sergeant. **Plague MEDIC:** cult 'blessings' (stimms and drugs) + knife, carapace chest, frag; inside the arch.
  - **Gardeners (cultist 18 good nurgle, basic):** #1 Agripinaa autogun + machete, flak, **rot-spore bottle** (arch nest); #2 machete + stub auto, rags (ground floor); **#3 stolen Ryza FLAMER** + trench club, gas mask (arch nest); #4 trench club, flak vest (ground floor); **#5 DUELIST** autopistol + chainsword, rags, STR+3 (ground floor, will push out); **#6 SNIPER** stolen Mezoa long-las + laspistol (roof); #7 sawn-off + 4 throwing knives, flak vest, Frenzon (arch nest); #8 autopistol + trench club, flak; found: jury-rigged autogun (arch nest); #9 autopistol + chainsword, rags (upper slit); #10 toxin needle-blade + stub revolver, carapace chest, **rot-spore bottle** (upper floor); **#11 DEMOLITIONS** demo satchel + detonator, rags (ground floor; mad enough to blow the arch); #12 4 throwing knives + sawn-off, flak vest, stimm (upper slit); **#13 SNIPER** scoped autogun + stub revolver (roof); **#14 BODYGUARD** heavy carapace, combat shotgun, shield (with the sergeant); #15 4 throwing knives, gas mask (ground floor); #16 autopistol + chain knife, gas mask (upper slit); #17 4 throwing knives + sawn-off, flak; found: officer's laspistol (ground floor); #18 Rashad axe, carapace chest (ground floor).

Melee ones on the ground floor.

He readies the breaching charge and kraks.

- **~0640: Kesh's reply** (astropathic, relayed by the Spire; terse): the Mart holds, good; **bring Thask, Ilsabet and the girl to the Rest yourself, under guard**; **Aldwyn comes for the rest in two days: hold the Mart and the prisoners till she does**; **the Sixth: say nothing of them on any open channel**; **all evidence (the jar, Thask's books, the manifests) sealed for her hands only**; **'I said never burn the Mart. A tenement burned. You'll explain it standing in front of me.'** (from 'Kesh may ask': the gunship crash).
- **~0645-0715: Tamsin read Ada's letters (d20 8: personal only) and the hangar clipboard (d20 3: maintenance and fuel only).** **The letters** (new names, checked unused): Ada and their son **Benno** (about four) live in the hab rings at **Mott's Landing**; Lenn sends money home from flying for 'the crane man'; she begs him to come home; the last letter, three months old, says the money stopped for a while and Benno has a cough. Nothing about Thask or the cult. **The clipboard:** the gunship's maintenance and fuel flimsies (the starboard intake coughing, rocket-pod rearms, fuel draws from the hangar drums); nothing about where it flew.
- **~0720:** Tamsin to Ollin: 'We're going to need a bigger ship... and some help for my quartermaster.' Ollin (in character, no roll): Mag Vashti's Void Exchange keeps its depot on **Vashti's Rock, a small Free Trader asteroid ~8 km off the Mart** (retconned at the player's suggestion from 'warehouses on the high side': a Free Trader keeps her own rock, her own guns, out of Voke's customs, which is why the rising never touched her; the high side's trade halls are Voke's merchant houses, shuttered through the rising) (she buys anything and brokers hulls); the Mart is full of dockers out of work after the rising; he'd like two strong backs and someone who can count.
- **~0725: Tamsin's plan: no new ship; upgrade the *Bright Light* instead, after the drop-off at Kesh's (Thask, Ilsabet, the girl), either before or after the hive city.** A refit is yard work (days to weeks); where and how much not set yet.
- **~0730:** Ollin's helpers: Joren (ex-docker) and Dannel once mended (Sallow will say how long); **for now Tamsin hires two porters off the dock ring**, Ollin to pick with him, going now. **Mira asleep (d20 11)**; Teo exhausted holding the ship. Ollin's way round it: have Teo set the *Bright Light* down at a dock ring berth (minutes; then he sleeps) and walk.
- **~0735:** Tamsin praised Teo's turret work; Teo (ex-void racer, smuggler's runner on the Tallow Run with a stubber on a hauler) wants a real ground-attack gun on the spine in the refit. Tamsin: air-to-ground missiles and auto turrets too. Teo said he could dock. **Docking at a dock ring berth (berth 12): d20 2 +4 DEX -2 (up all night) = 4 vs 12: failed: a hard bump into the berth's fender arm**: everyone aboard jolted awake; **a buckled hull plate on the port quarter (Ferro to check; cosmetic unless he says otherwise), the berth's docking arm bent** (the dockmaster wants paying). Docked ~0745.
- **~0750:** Tamsin to Teo: 'While drunk, huh?' Ferro sent to inspect. **Ferro's read (d20 11): cosmetic but a day's work** (the plate buckled, no breach; he and Dace can beat it out and reseat it; safe for the warp). **The berth 12 dockmaster: Gil Tannoch** (he/him; new name, checked unused): **businesslike (d20 12)**; the docking arm is the Mart's property: **300 Thrones** to straighten it (d6 3 x 100). **Tamsin paid 300 + 10 for Tannoch to keep the berth's work space clear and nobody touching the ship** (from the coin box: 25,218). Tannoch accepted, pleased.
- **~0805-0815: Tamsin and Ollin walk the ring to hire two porters** (Tamsin told Ollin his old Interrogator, Varsaine, barely slept). **The hiring post by berth 9** (a bench and a chalkboard under a dead crane; ~a dozen out-of-work dockers). **Candidates who step up (rolled; new names checked unused):** **Hask** (he/him): big (19), old and grey, steady hands; **honesty d20 1: a thief** (will pilfer from the hold if hired; hidden: an Insight/Perception read could catch it). **Brin** (he/him): big (15), ex-militia, lost his unit in the rising; honesty 11 (honest enough). **Oda** (she/her): wiry (6), prays to the Emperor under her breath; honesty 10. **Pruitt** (he/him): solid (12), brought his own hand-truck; honesty 14 (honest). Going rate for a docker's day on the Mart: ~3-5 Thrones (set). **Who can read and count (d20 11+): Oda only (12)**; Hask 10, Brin 9, Pruitt 3: no. Their pitches: Hask, thirty years on the ring, crane operator, never drops a load; Brin, militia-trained (lasgun), can carry and stand a watch; Oda, small and quick for crawlspaces and tight stacks, reads and tallies; Pruitt and his hand-truck 'Bess', a stacker who packs a hold tight. Asks (per day): Hask 5; Brin 4 (3 with food and a bunk); Oda 6 (5 + 1 for counting); Pruitt 4 and a hot meal.
- **~0820: HIRED: Pruitt at 4 a day** (and his hand-truck). **Oda offered 4 with food** (Tamsin: the quartermaster already counts; she doesn't look worth 5 for hauling): **her answer (d20 13): she counters**: 5 with food, and she'll keep a second tally of everything that goes in and out of the hold for nothing, 'so nobody walks off with your loot'. **Tamsin: 'Deal.' HIRED: Oda at 5 a day with food, plus the second tally.** Not hired: Hask (the thief) and Brin (ex-militia, crestfallen).
- **~0840:** Tamsin told Ollin to watch the two and check them every time they leave the ship ('their blood on your hands if they steal'); welcomed Oda and Pruitt to the Light (train and eat with the team; clean, organised guns sell better; steal and he'll come for them); slept. **~0840-1640 quiet (d20 4).** The Light slept till early afternoon, then light drill and cleaning. **Oda and Pruitt sorted most of the haul into the two containers** (Oda's tally slate matches Ollin's; nothing missing: honesty 10 and 14). **The hull plate not finished (d20 4): Ferro and Dace beat it out today; reseating tomorrow morning.** Sallow: Joren resting, fever down. Tamsin woke ~1640.
- **~1650: Tamsin's honours in the galley (the whole Light, crew, Oda, Pruitt, Dannel and Pell).** Kell (led Team 2; 'I just stood there and got shot'; the room drummed the tables; **named Most Valuable Frontline Warrior (MVFW)**, added after Tavi's turn). Tavi (the doors, the trap; 'right the first time'). **Morcant named Most Valuable Heavy (MVH).** **Sallow named Most Valuable Support (MVS).** **Corvane named Second Most Valuable Frontline Warrior (SMVFW).** Kell called him 'Lieutenant' (first time in the Light). **Nessa named Most Valuable Assassin (MVA)**; Tamsin called the Light her family. (Nessa owned to calling Dace her little brother; he hugged her.) **Sorel made a full Light-bringer: tag now 'Light-bringer, tracker'.** **Tey made a full Light-bringer: tag now 'Light-bringer, gunslinger'** (assumed from 'gunslinger in training'; the role's rules wait for the player's word). **Mira named Most Valuable Flyer (MVF)**; Tamsin promised her a new Valkyrie with air-to-ground weapons soon. **Teo named Second Most Valuable Flyer (SMVF)**; promised the *Bright Light* will be fitted with enough guns to make him 'the angel of death from above' (second angel to Varga). Ferro-Ashe commended (the Mart's plans and files, Hollis saved at Ustrennos, Corvane's sword, Varga's pack and gun link, bringing the Light closer to their weapons); **Tamsin promised to let him fight next time.** Ollin commended (awake longest, the overnight rekit, the coin box, the loot sort; 'a job that matters as much as a ground trooper'). **Dannel** honoured (his honour regained; a path to the Light): **his recruiting Will set now at 50%** (the floor: fought for the Light, went Down for it, his sister freed); **breaking point 50% (d31+19, hidden)**: not broken (Will must drop below it); **no will check from the honours** (a will check is an hour's push; the roll was voided before it was applied, the player's correction). Tamsin's Perception read 11+3 WIS+4 = 18: about 45-55% (he wants in; shame and Pell hold him back). **Joren** honoured in his absence: he'd shuffled up from the medicae and heard it from the doorway (Sallow furious).
- **~1740-2200:** the Light ate and trained (~4 h on the plan; Kell light; Joren and Dannel resting; Ferro on the hull). **Day 34 tally applied** (Tamsin STR 30.4 DEX 78.3 CON 23.9 INT 10.7 WIS 38.5 CHA 14.6; **Isk DEX 50.3: +5 now**; Tey drill day 19). **Session 10 XP: +18,000 (98,975): level 11**; Fighter 11's Extra Attack (2) confirmed by the player, then simplified: **+1 shot on every string and +1 swing on every chain**, whatever the weapon's limit. The player asked if the board was right: checked: Hollis's frags mostly wounded (the bell-man, the snipers) and others finished them (his one Down: the crit at the mast); Dace's Downs are none (his are surrenders forced on the Spire stair, the Ogryn stopped for Corvane, Dannel shielded at the mast). **Mission kill board (Downs, days 33-34, from the logs, approximate):** Tamsin ~38 + the gunship; Morcant ~25; Tey ~16; Varga ~14; Teo 9 (the turret pass); Corvane ~8; Kell 6; Sallow 6; Sorel 4; Nessa 3 (darts); Isk 3; Tavi 3; Brenner 2; Hollis 1 + the bell-man assist; Dace 0 (the Ogryn stopped; the walking wall); Mira 0 (the flying); Joren 0 (2 hits); Dannel 0 (1 hit). **Varga named Most Valuable Shooter (MVSH).** **Dace named 'the Protector'** (the Ogryn at the Spire pad; the walking wall). Hollis (the bell-man frag; ribbed for the cannery round). **Brenner named Second Most Valuable Support (SMVS)** ('Thorn'; the auspex reads). **Isk named Most Valuable Recon (MVR) and Second Most Valuable Shooter (SMVSH).** (Acronyms fixed by the player: shooters MVSH/SMVSH; Brenner's support SMVS; the clash scene retconned.)
- **The cannery (rolled ~0400): ~6 Tallow Boys + MARL TEGG** (here, d20 20): #1 heavy gunner (drum autogun), #2 breacher (boarding shield + sawn-off; found: another drum autogun), #3 chain-fed autopistol + shock baton, #4 combat knife; found: a stolen demo satchel, #5 GUNSLINGER (two Hammerfall revolvers), #6 torch-thrower (scrap flamer) + shank. **Pell Rusk is there** (d20 3). Team 2 en route through the Low Quay (~0405 arrival).
- **Hidden traits (rolled day 35 ~0345, in secret at the player's request; codex house rule 9, NPCs 1 roll at 50/50):** **Oda:** good, **Handy** (+1 on field repairs and fixing jams) (d2 1, d30 18). **Pruitt:** good, **Iron grip** (can't be disarmed by a solid hit; +1 on STR to hold on, grapple or keep hold of something; his hand-truck never gets away from him) (d2 1, d30 21). Show them in play, never name them.
- **Rot-spore gas (ruling, day 33):** CON save or sickened (drops a health step at the end of the fight unless given antitoxin); rebreathers and gas masks give advantage on the save (Tey and Sorel's filters count as masks).
- **Warp exit (pre-rolled 5, rough), USED ~1100 day 33:** the Light drops out into the stream of ships fleeing the Mart; a hauler scrapes the dorsal plating (cosmetic).
- **Faction wealth:** Mart cultists **Good** (74), rolled day 31.
- **Nemeses this mission:** **Kessick Vay rolled 18: he appears this mission.** Hessler rolled 15: not this time; he grows stronger (better gear or more followers). Details under "Nemeses".

- **Set day 36 (the leg-shot trooper of the 6th, broken a little by Tamsin's Intimidation 13+5 = 18 vs 17, narrow):** **the Mart was meant to be the 6th's port**: air plant, vox mast and customs house are what a regiment takes to hold a harbour; Gallowmere's grain passes through the Mart's customs to the sector, and the girl was to be shipped from there to the garden-world before the Lamps. **'You took a port. The garden has others.'** (Which other ports: not set.) The Light now knows this.
- **Set day 36 (the same trooper, Tamsin's truth-dart bluff 16+6 = 22, solid):** **what the Mart lets through that the 6th care for: OUT, the grain (Gallowmere's, seeded with the rot in the silos) and the jars, on to the sector; IN, 'beds': living people taken off passing ships through bought customs, ten Thrones a head, to grow more of the 6th's walkers** (the 'cold flickers'). He doesn't know the other ports or the Lamps ('I'm a trooper, not a colonel').
- **Set day 36 (40k lore, Darktide; the player named him):** **the Moebian 6th's commander is Captain Biron Wolfer, 'the Wolfer'** (with two lieutenants in the lore, the Carnak Twins; not met). **He wrote the green-sealed letters to Thask (the cramped soldier's hand, signed only with the gate) and pays Thask ten a head for the beds.** Confirmed to Tamsin by the leg-shot trooper (Intimidation 12+5 = 17 vs 17). Where Wolfer is now: not set.
- **Set day 36 (Aldwyn's share; 40k lore, Darktide):** the Moebian 6th were raised mostly on Atoma Prime and served long tours in the Fringe War at the edge of the Moebian Domain (heroes to Tertium); sent home to Atoma Prime to put down the Admonition cult in Hive Tertium, they were turned to Nurgle instead and now hold the hive with the cult (accounts differ on exactly how they fell; 'Darktide' is the game's name, not an in-world event: never use it in the fiction); their main strength is on Atoma Prime, in the hive Tertium, where **Inquisitor Grendyl's** agents fight them. Their leadership was taken prisoner once; **Wolfer's men attacked the transport and freed him.** Nobody in the Ordo expected them this far out: **that's why Kesh wants them kept off every open channel** (if they're here, they've spread, and someone is feeding them ships). What Kesh holds beyond that: not set.
- **Set day 36 (Aldwyn's past, rolled):** worst foe d6 2: **a heretek cult on a forge moon (Caddis Reach, campaign-made)**: tech-priests who traded the Machine God for the warp and walked their servitors at the line; **her scar is from a servitor's cutter arm that came through her helm there**. How much she shares d6 6: all of it, scar included. Beyond that she's fought cultists all her life ("your department was mine first"), witches, mutants, greenskins once. Kesh's history with her stays unset.
- **Set day 36 (Vashti's Rock, rolled):** Mag Vashti meets the Light herself (d6 4); **mood cold (d6 1)**: an Inquisition rosette can requisition a Free Trader's stock in its master's name, and the rising cost her the Mart's trade for days. She'll still deal, at Void Exchange prices (about double).
- **The 6th's three in the hold cage: Will (house rule 8; set day 36, retroactively, after the player asked; hidden, never told):** **the leg-shot trooper: Will 65% at capture** (a crushing defeat, his sergeant and squad dead, but a fanatic regiment), **breaks below 25%**; traits (secret): Void-sick. **The drum gunner: Will 85%, breaks below 15%**; traits: Hard of hearing, Sickly. **The plague medic: Will 100%, breaks below 5%** (a true believer: almost never); traits: Slow healer, Lucky. **Day 36 ~0941-0952, one session (the first roll is the session's will check; the other three rolls stand only as what was said in the moment): Intimidation 13+5 = 18, +1 the provost's manual (missed at the table, fixed) = 19: the trooper -11% (band 10-15, d6 2) = 54%. Not broken.** His slips (the port, the cargo, Wolfer) stand as said. With the gunner pressed against him and the medic watching, he won't talk in the cage; alone, maybe.
  - **Session 2 (~0956, the 'talking darts' threat with Nessa; corrected: a spoken threat is ordinary pressure, not a hard method, so no +5% and no risk rolls): will check Intimidation 4 + 1 CHA + 4 prof + 1 (Nessa and the dart) + 1 (the provost's manual, missed at the table) = 11: band 5-10% (d6 4) = 8% off each.** Trooper 54 → **46%** (breaks below 25); gunner 85 → **77%**; medic 100 → **92%**. Nobody broke. Sessions today: 2 of 3. **~0958: Tamsin to the medic: 'I should have figured a doctor would catch on...' (the bluff conceded): the medic named it a paralytic by the casing; the trooper steadied a little.**

## Entries

### Nemeses and witnesses

**The rule now (player, session 10):** no automatic rolls. After each fight, add who got away or was left alive and saw it (where, what they saw, how they feel) to the witness list below. Bring one back when the story fits; roll only when genuinely undecided.

**Witnesses:**
- **Crane Yard 6 (CY-6), east dock ring:** the Hookmen's hangar where the gunship was kept; the pilot's iron padlock key opens it. What's inside and whether Dagan Crale is there: not set (roll when the Light goes or the pilot talks).
- **The Hookmen gunship pilot (day 33, ~1223):** ran from the vox mast fight with the scav-rigged gunship's keys; saw Tamsin, the Light, the jumper and the charge down the road. Where the gunship is and what he does next: not set (the Light's Valkyrie has no rockets). The 8 who surrendered and 10 Down are prisoners.
- **The Spire pad prisoners (day 33, ~1107):** 8 Hookmen and Tallow Boys taken (#1, #6 Down; #2, #4, #9, #10, #11, #13 surrendered). They saw the white-trimmed grey plate, the white Valkyrie, Tamsin with the chainsword and machine pistol, the jumper, heard the call-and-answer ('WHAT ARE WE?' 'WE ARE THE LIGHT'). Bought gangers, not believers: scared, beaten, want to live. The far-roof three (#2 Badly hurt, #4 Hurt, #9 Fine) were left (Tamsin: 'they will probably retreat') and **climbed down out of sight (~1114)**: they saw the whole fight, including Tamsin killing the witch with a machine-pistol burst from the stair head, and will tell the gangs and Thask's people. The other five are held on the pad.

**Nemeses (made under the old rule):**

Rolled at the end of session 9 for the Spindle survivors (all +3 for Orne's killing). The bosun and the jumper died in the spoke before anyone came. Not nemeses: the two who fled with the vox man, and two of the cuffed men. (Old rule: rolled at each mission start, 17+ appears.)

- **Kessick Vay** (TALLY's vox man, Savlar Chem-Dog). Trait: **obsession**. He has hours of THE LIGHT channel recorded (voices, call signs, Brenner's 1005 line, Tamsin shouting the word) and studies them. He left Brask and sells what he knows to Thask's network at the Mart. THE LIGHT was re-keyed on days 27-28, so he can't listen in, but he knows the voices and habits. **Appears at the Mart (rolled 18).**
- **Hessler** (the tracker with the scoped autogun, cuffed at the lock). Brenner's shot in his side healed ugly. Trait: **a vow** on Orne's name to put Tamsin down; two Chem-Dogs who wouldn't go back to Brask follow him. Both watched Tamsin kill Orne after they'd surrendered. **Mart: 15, not this time; stronger next time.**
- Channel history: a spoof-tag cracked THE LIGHT on day 9 (re-keyed day 11); TALLY's vox man rode it on day 16 (Brenner caught a second carrier); re-keyed again days 27-28 (Brenner and Ferro, 12 vs 12).

### The gardener's guest

- A renegade marksman trained on an Exitus pattern (deserted or cast out of Temple training, never a full Vindicare), hired by Thask. She wanted Tamsin's face "to know at a distance". Her shield-breaker round is for someone who wears a field, and Tamsin now wears a refractor field. Target not decided; let it follow the story.
- The Exitus round the Light seized went to Kesh with Aldwyn on day 14; whether she has another is not set.
- **She is stranded on Ustrennos** (day 31, Hollen overheard it, rolled 20). She waited on "the high side" (the merchant lodgings at the top of the spine, above ring one) for the watcher's pict of Tamsin's face, meaning to sail home on Charter 7 at 0500 on day 30. Merrick took Charter 7 at 0400, her handler Hob is gone, and she knows the Light did it. Day 31: lying low, trying to find a way off (12).

### Kesh may ask

- **The gunship crash (day 33, ~1253):** Tamsin's shots blew up the Hookmen's gunship over the Mart and it fell burning onto an occupied tenement on the dock ring (Kesh: 'never burn the Mart'). It was about to rocket the Valkyrie (Mira and ten prisoners aboard).
- **Aldwyn's reports:** she called the Joss questioning "crude" (he nearly drowned), and she said she'd tell Kesh that Tamsin cut Nessa's collar.
- **Nessa's secret, leak path:** Holt and Brann (now with Kesh) heard Nessa say she ran Thask's back door. Tamsin kept it out of his report, writing only that the address "came from what he knew".
- **Brannick:** an Ordo rosette shook down a ring-four lender in public (day 31); Kesh or Aldwyn may hear of it.

### Kesh's prisoners

- Harrack believes Tamsin promised him "alive and no penalty"; he'll hear of the penal legion.
- Sly believes Harrack threw the bomb that killed Wren; in a legion together, Sly goes for him.
- Fenn (Will 19% when last seen, not broken) knows TALLY's home, the Weir.

### Ustrennos

- **Thask's "kept", in Merrick's cells:** **Pask Hollow** (cell 9; a believer; **Will 33%, breaks below 13%**; still knows how to reach the *Sable Margin* through the ring-four relay and Orne's next-call codes; Merrick works him quietly for the next Ordo courier). Ilen Marsk (broke; Pask brought her in after her son died of lung-rot). Deacon Orrin Selk (broke, Will 3%; fifteen years kept). Selk's register: 23 marked *passed*, most of them children; 3 *kept*.
- **Thask's reach into the cells:** Orsk (day 9) and Haddle (day 29) were both poisoned in Merrick's cells. The network can still reach in.
- **Brannick:** broken and stripped (day 31) but still knows Tamsin's face; he might sell it. Let it follow the story. He's a plain loan shark, not tied to G&S; his den is the Sump Lantern, by the 4-D hauler blocks.
- **Lusk:** pardoned until the Light returns, wearing Merrick's collar on ring four and guarding Mirri; to be judged on return.
- **Dekker:** the ring-four debt man who knew Tamsin's face (Hollen's bunk-mate); Tamsin thanked him on day 29.
- Haddle's circle and the watcher network are broken; Thask's network knew the courier hadn't left (Haddle's message).

### The Spindle after the trade

- Brask was honest (rolled 20): the *Sable Margin* is gone for good unless the story brings it back (~60 aboard, no paymaster now).
- TALLY's vox man (now Kessick Vay) and two others fled into the Spindle's maintenance ways. Other Down chem-dogs were left in Bay Two's spoke; three cuffed men at the far lock.
- **Orne's gold** (a strongbox, about 40 TV) was left in Bay Two's spoke; Brask's boat may go back for his men and find it first.
- **10 armed tripwire charges** still across Bay Two (12 laid, 2 went off). Bay Two's doors are breached.

### TALLY's home: the Weir

A hidden anchorage in Gallowmere's outer belt, a dead ore-processing platform. Orne's people go there after a bad night. Fenn knows; Sly and Harrack don't.

### The Bone Wardens

They lost a strike cruiser in this sector about 18 months ago, "out past a dead sun", and the wreck was never found; whoever armed TALLY found it (may tie to Velk: a dead sun, the seed went there 18 months ago). Returning their dead brother's boltgun through Kesh earns the Light a Chapter's remembrance: one day, one favour. **Their reply came (rolled day 36, d6 6), carried by Aldwyn: a bone-white token, a carved chevron on a cord, and three words: 'Returned is remembered.' Shown to any Bone Warden, it buys Tamsin a hearing and one favour, once.** (What favour, and when the Wardens come: not set.)

### Vhal Korrin compound 8

The marker is wrong. The real compound is two levels down from where it's marked; the marked spot is a hive-gang den that will fight anyone.

### Ferro-Ashe's heresy

Cast out of the Mechanicus for letting a cogitator learn on its own. He still keeps its core in a sealed case in the workshop.

### Varsaine

His own story is true as far as it goes: keep him honest but not fully forthcoming. Agreement: Tamsin looks his way before going off-script.

## Recruiting Wills (house rule 8)

- **Dannel Rusk (recruiting; set day 37 ~0836, hidden): starts 55%** (Tamsin's read since day 34: about half-willing; he wants in but won't ask: shame (the garden, the shot at Tamsin) and Pell hold him back); **breaking point 40%** (below it he genuinely wants in, says so, and means it). What gets in: Pell (her safety, her future, her looking up to him), being trusted with real work, the garden named for the lie it was; what doesn't: being shamed for the mast (he already shames himself). **~0836: 'I see bravery here very little; I saved your sister; what do you want to do now?' (Pell and his shame: gets in) Persuasion 4+2 = 6, -1%: Will 54%.** **~0837: 'I had an idea for your sister, and for you on my crew as well' (new: a future for Pell and a place for him: gets in) Persuasion 14+2 = 16, -8%: Will 46%.** **~0838: Pell safe at the Rest with Kesh's people, visits between missions (the concrete offer: gets in) Persuasion 14+2 = 16, -6%: Will 40%: exactly AT the breaking point, not below it; one more line that gets in breaks him.** **~0839: 'Doesn't matter if you shot at me; a good asset, a better extension of the family; I need hands on my ship, and someone to watch it when we're on a mission' (trusted with real work: gets in) Persuasion 19+2 = 21, -10%: Will 30%: BELOW 40: HE BREAKS (recruiting): he genuinely wants in, said so, and means it.**
- **Joren Brisk (recruiting; set day 37 ~0846, hidden): starts 60%** (grateful, honest, tough; but his mam in 4-E pulls him home, and he still thinks of himself as a docker who took bad coin, not a soldier); **breaking point 40%**. What gets in: his mam provided for (word to her, wages sent home), honest work with his hands, respect for the mast without pity; what doesn't: pity, or being told he's a hero. **~0846: the message to his mam promised, then 'How do you feel now, what do you want to do now?' (the player asked for the roll; after the promise, it gets in) Persuasion 14+2 = 16, -7%: Will 53%.** **~0847: 'If we go back to Ustrennos, we pick your mom up and take her to the Rest; she'll be safe there' (his mam: gets in) Persuasion 19+2 = 21, -10%: Will 43%: close to breaking.** **~0848: 'She's your mom; she'll be glad you've reformed' (gets in) Persuasion natural 1: nothing: Will 43%.** **~0849: 'Then tell her the truth, all of it; then she's safe and you can turn to the Light' (the truth, her own rule: gets in) Persuasion 7+2 = 9, -2%: Will 41%: one point off breaking.** **~0851: 'If you were to join, I wouldn't put you on the frontlines' (his 'I'm not a soldier': gets in) Persuasion 10+2 = 12, -5%: Will 36%: BELOW 40: HE BREAKS (recruiting): he genuinely wants in, said so, and means all of it.**
