## Day 41: Saint Ivaine's Rest (hidden; never read out)

- **The flight down (d20 natural 1: trouble; d6 6): the landing below Kesh's residence is DARK: Kesh's own warning sign** (as warp day 9: her lower windows dark). **Why (d6 1): cult watchers on the residence, for the Bloom.** **5 of them (d4+1 = 5), with a ROCKET TEAM (d6 6)** among them: rank-and-file gardeners with a pict-lens and a vox, and a two-man rocket team (launcher + loader, 3 rockets) on the roof of a pilgrim hostel ~300 m off the landing, overlooking the approach. Their aim: bring the Valk down on its landing approach and take the Bloom out of the wreck (she survives a crash better than they care; Thask too if he lives). Kesh's people spotted them watching; she killed the landing lights and the lower windows to warn Tamsin off the open approach; the private line was guarded speech ('nothing else on this line'). **Reception (d6 3): Sister Aldwyn and armsmen are waiting at the dark landing**, under cover.
- Kesh on the watchers (set ~1501): her people saw them arrive on the west roofs yesterday (day 40); she has let them watch, so they don't know they're seen; she means to take them, alive if she can. She knows there are 'about five' and a long case among them; she does not know it is a rocket team.
- **~1513 Tamsin asks Kesh to fund the ship's expansion (Persuasion: the player's d20 + 1, CHA 1 (the manual is for will checks only); +2 to the total for his record this season: the Mart's clean sweep, the prisoners and paper delivered, nobody dead in the tenement [RETCON day 42: one civilian died in the street (Chapter 76); the +2 stands on the other two counts: the Mart's clean sweep, and the prisoners and paper delivered]).** Tiers (hidden): **9 or less:** no Ordo money; she names a yard and a broker, and he pays. **10-14:** she matches what he puts in, Throne for Throne, up to the core refit (berths, hold, armoury). **15-19:** the Ordo pays the core refit and he pays for the guns (Teo's list). **20+:** the Ordo pays the core and half the guns, and she gets him a yard slot after the Lamps. Kesh's mood: cold but fair (d20 7).
- **The hostel roof (set ~1540, Sorel's look 17+5 = 22: he sees it all):** flat roof, a stairwell housing at the back (upslope side, door facing upslope), a water tank on stilts, laundry lines, the blue prayer lamps along the front parapet (facing down the slope to the landing). **The five:** two at the front parapet with the pict-lens on a little tripod and a vox set (watching the landing); **the rocket team, two, by the water tank: the long case open, a tube launcher assembled and three rockets laid out on a cloth**, one man lying beside it with his eye on the landing approach; one by the stairwell door, sitting with his back to the housing, a stub pistol in his lap (their door guard, half asleep). Sidearms: stub pistols on the guard and one parapet watcher; knives on all. They are gardeners (rank-and-file); the launcher man is the only one who has done this before.
- ~1542 The approach: the door guard dozes facing up the slope beside the stairwell door; his Perception 17 + 0 - 5 (dozing) = **12**. Team Stealth: Nessa 6+7 = 13 (ok), Sorel 15+3 = 18 (ok), **Tey 2+5 = 7 (fails: a scuff; the guard stirs and looks toward Tey)**. Tamsin's d20+10 decides whether he's on the guard unseen as he wakes.
- ~1543 Did the tile clack carry to the four on the roof (d20 - 2 vs 15; the choir, facing away)? Parapet A 10-2 = 8, parapet B 12-2 = 10, launcher man 11-2 = 9, loader 10-2 = 8: **nobody heard.** Distances from the housing: the water tank and the rocket team ~10 m; the front parapet ~20 m.
- ~1544 (after the retcon): the launcher man Down (needle, black-tip, CON 15 vs 17). The loader didn't notice (13 vs 15). **The loader's Perception vs Tamsin's approach: 12.** The parapet two still facing the landing.
- ~1546 Overheard at the parapet: 'Prophet said the flower comes to the Lady's house.' **'The Prophet' is new (said in play; not yet defined): whoever sent them and knew Maren would be brought to Kesh. To be settled when a watcher is questioned.** Parapet Perception vs Tamsin's approach (d20 - 2, eyes on the landing): **A (left) 7; B (right, stub pistol) 15.** Tie = still unaware.
- ~1547 B's struggle against Tamsin's grab: 9 + 1 - 2 (surprised) = **8**.
- **B (the parapet watcher held at knifepoint): Will set 55% (a quick surrender after watching four of his go down without a sound); breaking point 40% (a frightened believer, not a fanatic).** ~1548 'Who is in charge?' (Intimidation, knife at the throat: gets in): 12+6 = 18: -7% (d3 2 in the 6-8 band) -> **48%**. Unbroken: he gives what feels safe: the launcher man gives the orders here; above that, he'll only say 'the Prophet' if pushed.
- **The truth behind the watchers (set ~1655; not yet known to anyone in play):** **the leak** was Kesh's own astropathic reply of day 34 (relayed through the Spire's astropath on Torvane's Mart): a gardener in Voke's household (the astropath's scribe) copied 'bring Thask, Ilsabet and the girl to the Rest' and the garden sent word ahead on a fast pilgrim courier. **'The Prophet':** a cult preacher on the Rest itself, posing as a wandering pilgrim-prophet in the lower ring's pilgrim halls, preaching 'the garden' dressed as the Emperor's mercy; he gathered the watchers (two locals, three off the courier) and had the launcher brought in among pilgrim crates. He is on the Rest now.
- **The tube man:** an ex-Guard deserter (a rocket team man in a regiment he calls 'a dead one'); **Will 85%, breaking point 15%** (hard; has been questioned before by worse). **Kesh's first session with him (d20 natural 1): little: no name, no regiment, nothing.** She moves on to the others; **B (Will 48%, breaks below 40%) is the soft one**, and he knows the Prophet's face and the hall where he preaches; he does not know about the leak.
- **~1702 B broken.** Tamsin: 'you're young... it's over for you unless you cave... I already know who you work for... so what was the plan' (Intimidation, the bluff as new leverage: gets in): **natural 20 + 6 = 26: the 9-10% band (d3 3: 10%), doubled by the natural 20: -20% -> 28%: BROKEN (below 40).** He answers truthfully what he knows. **B is Wenn, 20, a hostel kitchen hand on the Rest (a local, like the door man; the tube man, the loader and A came yesterday on the courier).** What he knows: **the Prophet** preaches at the evening office in the long pilgrim hall on the lower ring, behind the candle market: a tall thin man in grey sackcloth, barefoot, a staff wound with dried flowers, a soft voice; he preaches 'the garden' as the Emperor's mercy blooming; he picked Wenn and the door man out of the crowd weeks ago, fed them, blessed them; three days ago he said the Lady would bring the flower home to her house, and set them to watch; the launcher came in a pilgrim crate. **How the Prophet knew: Wenn doesn't know** ('he knew; the garden tells him'). He doesn't know the leak.
- **~1704 Wenn (broken) on where and what:** the Prophet never misses the **evening office at the eighteenth bell (1800)** in the long pilgrim hall behind the candle market; where he sleeps, Wenn doesn't know (he comes and goes). **Wenn was to go down at the evening office and tell him 'still dark'**; if nobody comes, the Prophet may take it as a warning (roll when it matters). **The objective:** when the white bird came in, the tube man fires into its engines over the landing; they take the flower out of the wreck alive and carry her down to the Prophet; **the courier that brought the three is still at the pilgrim berths, waiting to carry her 'to the real garden'** (Wenn doesn't know its name or berth). Everyone else in the wreck didn't matter.
- **The Prophet's face (set ~1705, from Wenn):** fifties; head shaved but for a thin ring of white hair; very pale grey eyes; always half-smiling; a ring of dried flowers round his neck as well as on the staff; **the two smallest fingers of his left hand gone** (old, healed); a faint sweet smell about him (like the Mart's bloom jars, though Wenn wouldn't know that).
- ~1753 The long pilgrim hall: a long nave, benches, candle stands, an Ecclesiarchy priest at the altar for the evening office at the 18th bell; **only ~50 pilgrims tonight (d6 1): a thin crowd, harder to hide in.** The Prophet sits on the steps of the side chapel halfway down the left aisle with a small circle of listeners; **two devotees close at his side (d4-1 = 2), robed like pilgrims, hands in their sleeves (knives); they watch the room while he talks.**
- ~1755 Wenn's report: his Deception 10 + 0 + 2 (Aldwyn's walk-through) = 12 vs the Prophet's Insight 6 + 3 = 9: **believed.** The Prophet suspects nothing tonight. He sits with his back to the side chapel's steps, facing the aisle: no clean back shot while he sits.
- ~1756 The distraction: **both watchers went (d6 6)** to the far side. The Prophet's Perception vs Tamsin's approach: 16 + 3 - 5 (watching the commotion) = **14**. If the tracker dart hits, his WIS 15 check to feel it: d20 + 3.
- ~1830 After the office (d6 6): **the Prophet goes to the courier first**, with his two watchers, out by the left-hand door, through the candle market and down the pilgrim stair toward the lower quay of the pilgrim berths. **The courier: the *Lamb of Ivaine*, a small grey pilgrim courier at a lower-quay berth; crew and guards aboard to be rolled when it matters.** Kesh's armsmen are going through the pilgrim berths tonight: risk of the Prophet seeing them (roll when they get close).
- ~1832 The tail: the watchers checking behind them: best 15 + 1 = **16**. Nessa 4 + 7 + 2 (a street back on the tracker) = 13: **she's seen** (the purse woman from the hall, following). Their reaction to roll after Tamsin's Stealth.
- ~1833 Tamsin's tail 19 + 12 = 31: unseen. **The watcher who spotted Nessa (d6 5): told the Prophet; they hurry.** The Prophet now uneasy: heading straight for the *Lamb of Ivaine* on the lower quay; if nothing stops him he boards and the courier makes ready to cast off (roll its readiness when they get there).
- ~1834 Tamsin closing in on the stair (no street-back bonus now): the watchers looking for a tail, best 19 + 1 + 2 (alert) = **22**. Tamsin's d20 + 10 needs 22.
- ~1836 Tamsin's tail 17 + 10 = 27 vs 22: unseen. **The *Lamb of Ivaine* (d6 5): engines warm, can cast off in minutes. Aboard: 3 crew (d4+1 = 3): a pilot in the cockpit, two hands (one at the gangway with a stub autopistol under his coat, one on the mooring clamps).** Kesh's armsmen not on this quay (d6 3). The tracker reaches ~1 km: once the courier clears the station, it's gone.
- ~1837 Tamsin's fast quiet push (on his toes, drawing Last Word; -2 for the speed): the watchers and the gangway hand at the gangway, best **20 + 1 = 21** (the rear watcher looking back up the quay). Tamsin's d20 + 8 needs 21.
- **~1838 What they carry (hidden; set now; Tamsin's Perception d20 + 8 sees each item at or above its number):** the two watchers: long knives in their sleeves (10), no armour, sackcloth only (10). The gangway hand: a stub autopistol under his coat (12), a flak vest under it (15). The clamp hand: a heavy wrench in his hand (5), a knife at his belt (12). **The Prophet:** the staff's foot is a sharpened iron ferrule (15); **a small stoppered glass vial on a cord at his chest under the sackcloth, the sweet smell: bloom rot (18)**; no armour. The pilot inside: unseen.
- ~1838 Tamsin opens fire from hiding (Extra Attack: three shots this beat; surprise +2 on all three, they haven't reacted yet). Hidden numbers: **the Prophet's leg 15** (a called shot on a man climbing a gangway, ~12 m); **the gangway hand 12** (flak: a hit still drops him one step); **a watcher 12**. A hit drops one step (Fine, Hurt, Badly hurt, Down). The Prophet: a leg hit stops him climbing (he falls back down the gangway or sprawls at the hatch: d6 when it lands).
- **The Prophet (Tamsin's to question, day 41 evening): Will 95%, breaking point 10%** (a fanatic: near-impossible by pressure alone; needs real leverage). Leverage he doesn't know Tamsin has: Wenn broke and told everything (his hall, his face, the courier); the five on the roof all taken alive; the courier lanced; **the bloom vial found on him** (Kesh's armsmen searched him: a stoppered glass vial on a cord, sweet-smelling: the Mart's bloom rot); the tracker dart in his neck (he never felt it). **What he knows:** the leak (a gardener in Voke's house: the astropath's scribe, by name **Corlis**, sent word by a fast pilgrim courier on day 35); the garden's word went out from the Mart to the Rest and to Gallowmere; at the Harvest Lamps the garden 'opens' at the grain silos of Gallowmere, and its saint (the Bloom) was to be there; he doesn't know Wolfer personally (the Sixth's officer is a name to him). What the vial is for: a 'last mercy' if the garden's enemies take him: break it in a crowd.
- ~1932 The Prophet: 'sent them to their deaths... the Sixth cut back... we know where Wolfer is (a bluff)... you have failed your grandfather' (Intimidation; the name Wolfer and 'grandfather' are new and get in): 5 + 6 = 11: -5% (10-14 band, d3 3) -> **90%**. A ripple at 'Wolfer' (he knows the name as the Sixth's officer, not the man); 'grandfather' he takes as recognition, not a wound.
- ~1933 'Wolfer's corpse on my doorstep... your missiles... your team on a roof... a man you thought your own came up to you: is it still dark, or was that a lie?' (Intimidation; Wenn's lie, the roof five and the launcher are real, new leverage): 19 + 6 = 25: -9% (20+ band, d3 2) -> **81%**. The first real crack: the smile went; he hadn't known the roof was taken. He is bluffing with 'the garden already knows'.
- ~1935 The names (Voke? Oda? Pruitt? a dockmaster?): no will check (the pressure is the Wenn card again, and endless captivity doesn't frighten him), but a read. **Oculine with the names as a hook: 15 + 5 + 2 = 22 vs 15: she catches it at 'Voke': a tower with a singing room (the Spire's astropath choir), a thin man with ink on his fingers copying out words by lamplight; not Voke himself; someone in his house.** The Prophet's face as the names went by: **11** (Tamsin's Insight d20 + 4 vs 11: he relaxes at Oda and Pruitt and the dockmaster; tightens at Voke).
- ~1936 'I appreciate your service to the Emperor' (he's just given up the garden's man in Voke's house: new): 18 + 6 = 24: -9% (d3 1) -> **72%**. Unbroken. In zeal he says what he's proud of: the missiles were for the white bird; the girl would have gone home to the garden, to Gallowmere, to open at the Harvest Lamps, and 'every field would have drunk from her'. (True as far as it goes; not the dormant-rot mechanism, not her birth.)
- ~1937 'She was born to be saved... trained to fight for the Emperor... she'll kill Wolfer with me... the choir on the Mart, not many left, I know which one, nosy... you'll be the reason for his death' (Intimidation; new): **3 + 6 = 9: -2% -> 70%.** It got in but barely moved him; he laughed at the Sister idea. ('I know which one' is a bluff; Tamsin doesn't know the scribe's name, Corlis.)
- ~1939 The alphabet read (the scribe is Corlis: C): the Prophet now guards body and face (he learned from the names): **22**. Tamsin's Insight d20 + 4 needs 22.
- ~1941 'You lie... it's a C, isn't it' (a bluff that happens to be right: Corlis): Deception will check 6 + 2 = 8: -2% -> **68%**. His face held; **Oculine felt the ripple at C (12 + 7 = 19 vs 15)**: confirmed to Tamsin that the letter landed.
- **Oculine's seeing of Maren (day 41 ~1945-2000; her word to Kesh tomorrow):** no taint of the warp in her, no mark, no psyker's spark; 'loud like a bell' is the rarest thing Oculine knows: a soul the warp slides off. **And in her blood the rot, asleep, that doesn't touch her** (it never has: the infant on the quarantined hauler). Oculine tells Tamsin tonight only: 'Nothing in her that shouldn't be there. And something I've seen once before in sixty years.' The rest is for the Lady.

## Day 42 (hidden; never read out)

- **Moll's answer by morning (d6 4): she took the Spire's astropath and four scribes into her cells; a fifth scribe, thin, inky fingers, CORLIS, was gone before her men got there**: his cot cold, his slate wiped, a pilgrim courier's passage chit in his things (outbound, the day after the Light jumped). Kesh tells Tamsin at the briefing: 'one of them ran'; the name Corlis comes from the other scribes. Where he went: toward Gallowmere (not yet known in play).
- **The hour before the briefing (d20 3: something happens; d6 5: an astropathic message, bad news; d6 3: Gallowmere).** ~1040 a sealed slate from the Rest's choir to Kesh: **Gallowmere's choir has stopped answering** (Kesh's routine query out day 39; nothing back; the Rest's relay confirms no traffic from Gallowmere's astropaths in four days). Why: not set (let it follow the story).
- **Kesh's mood today (d20 2): foul** (the Gallowmere silence on top of the leak). Clipped, short-tempered; not at Tamsin.
- **Corlis's chit (d6 3): no destination on it**: just 'outbound, pilgrim courier *Pilgrim's Due*', the day after the Light jumped. Moll has the chit. (Hidden: toward Gallowmere.)
- **Kesh's Gallowmere intel (d20 4): poor.** She has the rumours (barges in rotten, crews sick, the three circles in a hold), the silos, the Lamps (~day 55), the old GALLOWMERE HIGH ORBIT vox tags, what the Prophet said. No map of where the garden sits, no numbers, nothing on the governor.
- **Her help (d6 4): papers and a name.** An Administratum tithe-assessors' warrant (cover for the Light) and one informant on Gallowmere: **a tithe-reeve at the main grain port** (name to be given in play); **last report ~20 days ago (d6 3): within the usual cadence, not yet overdue.**
- **~1108 Tamsin asks again about Maren and the Schola. Kesh's answer: still not yet, after the Lamps. How much of Oculine's read she shares (d6 3, table: 1-2 nothing, 3-4 a hint, 5-6 all): a hint.** Her chirurgeon drew Maren's blood last night (set now); 'something in that blood I want understood' before she goes near a Schola; not tainted, not a witch. She does NOT say the rot sleeps in it.
- ~1818 (hidden) Tamsin calls Varga and Nessa back ('I think you might want to reconsider'): Persuasion (not a will check: d20 + 1). Hidden numbers: **Nessa 11, Varga 13** (one roll against both).

## Day 42 evening: TTT, game 1 (hidden; never read out)

- Players in order (d9): 1 Tamsin, 2 Dace, 3 Brenner, 4 Isk, 5 Corvane, 6 Sorel, 7 Tey, 8 Nessa, 9 Varga. Joren the deck holder; Ollin the armoury shop (5 Thrones = a laspistol with a training pack).
- **Cards (d9 rolls 3, 8, 7): TRAITORS (jokers): BRENNER and NESSA. SHERIFF (king): TEY** (his pistol carried openly). **Tamsin: a number card, innocent.** Everyone else innocent: Dace, Isk, Corvane, Sorel, Varga.
- **The Thrones (20 from Tamsin's purse, 2 a spot; d16 on the clearing-game spot list): shrine 2, spare cabin 2, range 2, medicae 2, galley 2, Tamsin's cabin 2, Kell's cabin 2, Varga's cabin 2, bunk room 4.** None in the containers, mat, old cargo office, armoury, briefing room or bridge.
- ~1838 Tamsin to Nessa: 'You're an innocent, aren't you?' Perception (d20 + 8) to spot the knife on her vs **her concealment 7 + 7 = 14**.
- ~1838 Perception 11 + 8 = 19 vs 14: **Tamsin spots it**: the training knife tucked flat in her boot top, under the trouser leg (she keeps that leg back).
- ~1839 Tamsin to Nessa: 'I'll keep it interesting...' (kept her secret). **Nessa's read (d20 16 + 2 = 18 vs 12): she knows he's seen it.** She takes it as him playing along, but marks him: he's the one who can sell her out. Opening moves: Dace sticks by Tey (the sheriff); Brenner aft toward the armoury and mat (FIX: no limp, his ankle was down by day 39; and the bunk room is forward, not aft); Varga out alone toward the cabins; Corvane and Sorel together toward the hold; Nessa drifts out after Brenner.
- ~1841 The door that clicked (d3 3): **Varga is in the spare cabin**, hiding: **4 + d20 14 = 18**. She hasn't found its 2 Thrones yet (d6 3); they're tucked under the mattress edge: **Perception 10** to find.
- ~1841 Aft (d6 2: the traitors move): **Nessa on Sorel in the hold: Stealth 17 + 7 = 24 vs his Perception 14 + 5 = 19: touched, SOREL DOWN. Brenner on Corvane: 14 + 2 = 16 vs 2 + 2 = 4: touched, CORVANE DOWN.** Both sitting in the hold by the range lanes, silent. Nobody forward heard anything. **Left: Tamsin, Isk, Varga, Tey (sheriff), Dace; traitors Brenner, Nessa.**
- ~1842 Tamsin's Perception 9 + 8 = 17: finds the 2 Thrones (10); **misses Varga (18), by one** (she's in the tall locker, door pulled to).
- ~1844 Varga's read on Tamsin (he's telling the truth): 11 + 2 = 13 vs 10: **she believes him.**
- ~1845 Traitors' plan (d6 3: forward, hunting singles): Brenner and Nessa leave the hold, coming forward through the mat and armoury toward the galley (the only way forward). Isk and Varga reach the galley first. Bunk room Thrones: 4, Perception 10.
- ~1846 The galley: Varga and Isk told Tey (Tamsin saw a knife in Nessa's boot); Tey drew on Nessa as she and Brenner came in from aft. **Nessa (d6 6) went for Tey; Tey's shot 9 + 5 DEX + 3 gunslinger + 2 pistol focus = 19 vs 12: NESSA DOWN. Brenner (d6 4) struck at Tey in the same moment: 7 + 2 = 9 vs Tey's notice 12 + 1 - 2 = 11: seen; Tey's second shot 12 + 5 + 3 + 2 + 2 chain - 1 swap = 23 vs 10: BRENNER DOWN.** Both traitors down: **INNOCENTS WIN game 1.**
- **TTT game 2 (dealt ~1852):** cards (d9 1, 3, 5): **TRAITORS: TAMSIN and BRENNER. SHERIFF: CORVANE.** Innocent: Dace, Isk, Sorel, Tey, Nessa, Varga. Thrones (20, d16; the armoury roll rerolled to 15): **medicae 4, old cargo office 4, Kell's cabin 6, mat 2, range 2, bridge 2.**
- ~1853 Tamsin's knife deep in his jacket: Sleight of Hand 17 + 8 = **25**. Nessa came to check him (d6 4): her look 11 + 3 = 14: **sees nothing.** She asks him to his face (his answer: Deception if he lies). Opening moves: Dace by Corvane (the sheriff); Isk and Sorel together forward; Tey and Varga together aft; Brenner aft alone.
- ~1854 Tamsin's lie to Nessa ('Seems so...'): Deception d20 + 1 vs **her Insight 16 + 3 = 19**.
- ~1855 Deception **natural 20 + 1 = 21 vs 19: Nessa believes him** (innocent). She stays near Corvane (d6 3). Tey and Varga in the medicae (d4 2), searching for its 4 Thrones. Brenner (d6 5) waiting by the stair for a lone one.
- ~1856 Tamsin sneaks to the medicae doorway to kill the lights: Stealth d20 + 10 vs **Tey 20 + 1 - 2 (searching) = 19** (Jumpy, natural 20), **Varga 7 + 2 - 2 = 5**. A tie = unaware. (Lights off in one room: allowed; the game's 'lights on' meant the ship's.)
- ~1857 As Tamsin slips in: **Varga (d6 4) shouts for the sheriff**: 'CORVANE! MEDICAE! Someone killed the lights!' Corvane and Dace (and Nessa) ~10 m away in the galley: at the hatch in ~3 seconds. Listens in the dark if needed: **Tey 8 + 1 + 2 = 11, Varga 3 + 2 + 2 = 7.**
- ~1857 Tamsin rushes Tey in the dark (Tey's yelp gave his spot): knife d20 + 12 - 2 (dark, by sound) vs **Tey's dodge 12 + 5 + 1 (Light on his feet) - 4 (blind) = 14**; a tie lands. Then the re-hide: Sleight of Hand d20 + 8 - 2 (rushed), vs Corvane's look at the door.
- ~1857 Knife 14 + 12 - 2 = 24 vs 14: **TEY DOWN.** Re-hide 16 + 8 - 2 = **22**. Corvane at the door, lights on; his look 12 + 2 = 14: **doesn't see the knife.** Varga (behind the far cot) heard someone rush in from the door after the lights went; didn't see who.
- ~1858 Tamsin's story (Varga by the lights; a figure stabbed Tey beside him). Varga contradicts it (she was at the far cots; the lights went from the door; nobody was in the room before). Nessa stays silent (d6 2). **Corvane's Insight 13 + 2 + 2 (Varga's word) = 17.** Tamsin's Deception d20 + 1: **18+ he believes Tamsin** (and eyes Varga); **17 unsure, shoots nobody**; **16 or less: he shoots Tamsin.**
- ~1858 Tamsin's added argument (Tey's his boy, why would he; Varga only joined to get him: 'Only if I get to do it twice', heard by all): **+2 (DM's call, a real point Corvane saw)**. Deception 15 + 1 + 2 = **18 vs 17: Corvane believes him.** Corvane (d6 3) **shoots Varga: 10 + 4 = 14 vs 10: hit. VARGA DOWN. She was innocent: CORVANE DOWN TOO (the rule); his pistol on the deck by the doorway.** Everyone now knows Varga told the truth, so Tamsin lied.
- ~1859 Tamsin's long lunge at Dace (4 m): knife d20 + 12 - 2 (the long lunge) vs **10** (Dace (d6 2) bends for the gun, no dodge). **Nessa dives for the gun too: 15 + 5 = 20 vs Dace's grab 3 + 2 = 5: Nessa gets the pistol** (her shot comes next beat).
- ~1859 Tamsin carries the lunge into a swing at Nessa (1.5 m, his blade already moving: he goes first): knife d20 + 12 vs **her dodge 3 + 5 - 2 (rising off her knees, two hands on the gun) = 6**; a tie lands. If he misses: **her shot 11 + 5 = 16 vs 10, hits.**
- ~1859 Knife 10 + 12 = 22 vs 6: **NESSA DOWN**; the pistol clattered to the deck. Isk and Sorel (d6 5) heard it and hold forward, wary. **Left: traitors Tamsin and Brenner (at the stair); innocents Isk and Sorel (forward).**
- ~1900 Tamsin with the sheriff's pistol, into the corridor, looking round: Perception d20 + 8. **Brenner tucked at the head of the stair: hide 8 + 2 = 10.** **Isk and Sorel on the bridge (d4 4)**, door shut, out of sight from the corridor.
- ~1903 The bridge (Mira on watch at the helm, not playing): **Isk hide 15 + 5 = 20** (flat under the forward nav console); **Sorel 13 + 3 = 16** (pressed into the shadow behind the viewport frame, port side). **Brenner's search 17 + 3 = 20: finds both** (a tie finds).
- ~1904 Asked to give up: **Sorel (d6 4) and Isk (d6 4) both break for the door** together: Sorel fast from port; Isk scrambling out from under the console (a beat slower).
- ~1904 Tamsin's hip shots with the training pistol (no hair trigger, no laser; hidden number 12, running targets at 2-3 m): **Sorel 12 + 12 = 24: hit, down. Isk 15 + 12 + 2 chain - 1 swap = 28: hit, down. TRAITORS WIN game 2** (Tamsin and Brenner the last two).

## Day 43 (hidden; never read out)

- **The night (d20 4: something; d6 1: a Geller flicker; d6 1: something brushes through; d4 1: ~0110).** The field stutters for ~20 seconds: lights dim to ember, the hum drops, frost creeps on the inner bulkheads; in each sleeper's ear, a whisper in a loved one's voice. **In Tamsin's cabin: frost spreads across Maren's page of stars, and a child's voice in the dark says 'Are you coming back?'** Ferro re-seats the field from the Geller node. **Everyone aboard: a save vs fear/the warp, d20 + WIS mod (+1 Brave, +1 Pious, advantage with a purity seal) vs 13: fail = shaken (-1 on all rolls till they've slept again; bad dreams).** Teo on the bridge watch.
- ~0110 the saves (vs 13; WIS mod; Brave +1 Kell; Pious +1 Sallow; Tey advantage, his sealed hellpistol): **Tamsin natural 20 (adv 8/20) + 4 + 1 = 25: steady; he can tell the voice is not Maren's.** Pass: Kell 10+2+1 = 13, Varga 21, Dace 15, Hollis 16, Isk 22, Corvane 15, Nessa 17, Tey 17, Joren 16. **SHAKEN (-1 on all rolls till they've slept again; bad dreams): Brenner 4, Tavi 6, Morcant 4, Sallow 12, Sorel 10, Mira 11, Teo 5 (on the bridge watch), Ferro 6 (still re-seats the field, slower: ~40 s), Ollin 8, Dannel 2.**
- ~0111 (hidden) The bunk room as Tamsin arrives (~10 s after the shout): Morcant (shaken) up on his bunk shouting at a corner; Tavi curled on his bunk, hands over his ears; Dannel (shaken) on the deck whispering 'Mam?'; Brenner (shaken) sitting rigid; Sorel (shaken) on his knees, a Valhallan prayer through his teeth; Hollis, Isk, Corvane, Nessa, Tey, Dace (passed) up and trying to calm them. Frost on the racks. The whispers go on till the field comes back (~30 s more). Kell and Varga out of their cabins into the corridor.
- ~0111 Tamsin's rally on Morcant ('BIG GUY, SNAP OUT OF IT! I'M HERE!'): Intimidation (not a will check) d20 + 6 (CHA 2 + proficiency 4) vs **12**: success clears Morcant's shaken now; a natural 20 steadies the whole room.
- ~0111 Rally: Intimidation 16 + 6 = 22 vs 12: **Morcant snaps out (shaken cleared).** ~15 s left till the field comes back.
- ~0112 Tamsin's rally on Dannel ('IT'S NOT REAL! STAY HERE WITH ME!'): Intimidation d20 + 6 vs **14** (his save was the worst on the ship, and the voice wears his dead mam's). The field comes back right after this.
- ~0112 Rally: 14 + 6 = 20 vs 14: **Dannel snaps out (shaken cleared).** ~0113 **the field back** (Ferro): hum, lights, frost to water, the whispers gone. Still shaken till they sleep: Tavi, Brenner, Sorel, Sallow, Mira, Teo, Ferro, Ollin.
- ~0114: Tamsin, chainsword running, shouted up at the thing beyond the hull: 'DEAD! You hear me? That's it! You want to try and stop us getting to them... they're DEAD!' Nothing answered. Morcant bellowed it with him. Isk, quietly: it's gone; don't give it your voice. Sorel agreed. Kell: 'Boss. The sword.'
- **The watch (Tamsin outside the bunk room from ~0118; Night owl: +1 on the watch, no penalty for the short sleep). Rest of the night d20 1: something; d6 4: Ferro's report; d4 2: ~0340.** **Ferro's finding (d6 2): the field did not fail; it was PRESSED FROM OUTSIDE**, at one point on the hull's aft quarter, like a hand on glass; **and the pressure has not gone: faint, keeping pace with the ship.** Something in the warp is following the *Bright Light*. What it is: not set (let the story decide). **Each warp night left (days 43, 44, 45) d20: 1-5 it tries again**; **next night 1-7** (Tamsin answered it aloud: 'they feed on being spoken to'). Ferro can harden the node (a rite, a few hours, his INT; shaken -1 till he sleeps): success makes it 1-3; a purity seal on the node gives the crew advantage on the save.
- ~0348 Tamsin tries to commune with Shameholder (not a tech-priest; by feel and sound): **WIS d20 + 4 vs 15.** Success: he feels its mood: hunger, and its old revulsion (it hated the plague things on the *Widow*), turned aft toward where the field was pressed (a hint: what follows is rot-kin). Natural 20: the teeth twitch toward the aft quarter on their own. Fail: a machine's hum, nothing more.
- **Tamsin's promise to Shameholder (~0352): it cuts through the first one; he'll sate both their rages at the end of this fight.** Kept: the spirit's favour (DM: e.g. once, a natural 1 in overdrive doesn't burn the motor). Broken: it sulks (-1 on its swings till fed).
- **Ferro's warding rite (~0350-0610): natural 1 + 4 (INT) - 1 (shaken) = 4 vs 15: FAILED.** At the last verse the trace spiked (as if it watched), and a warding coupling cracked; Ferro stopped and patched it. **The node is no better and no worse: tonight still 1-7.** **Vigil's seal IS on the node: the crew get advantage on the save if it tries again.** Ferro can try the rite again after he has slept (shaken clears).
- **Ferro's second rite (from ~1300, done ~1630): 7 + 4 = 11 vs 15: FAILS** (no damage this time; the trace didn't spike). Tonight still 1-7; the seal still gives the crew advantage. Another try tomorrow; help from someone who knows the litany (or Tamsin holding the knife to the rune) could give advantage.
- **~1720 on the mat (d20 3: something; d6 3: Dannel comes to talk; d4 3: a memory from the garden).** Last night's voice shook loose something: as a boy in Thask's garden he carried crates to the *Hesk Tender*'s crew, and they always said they were going **'back to the Ninth'**, like a place; sweet dust on their boots; once a crate stencilled **'G-IX'**. **True (set now): the garden's heart on Gallowmere is in the Ninth tithe-district, Silo Complex IX.** Dannel doesn't know what it means. (Varrow, a tithe-reeve, would know the districts.)
- **Night of day 43 (d20 14 vs 1-7): it does NOT try tonight.** Quiet.

## Day 44 (hidden; never read out)

- **The third warding rite (~0610-0815), Tamsin assisting.** At the last verse Tamsin sets the knife's Geller-scorched tip into the node's rune and holds, and the trace spikes (it watches again). **Tamsin: WIS d20 + 4 vs 13 to hold steady through the spike: success gives Ferro advantage; a natural 20 also counts as the rite taking outright. Ferro: d20 + 4 (INT) vs 15.** Success: the node hardened, each night's chance drops to 1-3. Fail: as it was (1-5 a night; the 'answered' bonus was for the one night only).
- ~0815 Tamsin held through the spike: WIS 13 + 4 = 17 vs 13: Ferro gets advantage. **Ferro's rite: 16/18 + 4 = 22 vs 15: THE NODE IS HARDENED.** Each warp night left: **1-3** (from tonight). The trace is still there, faint, keeping pace.
- **~1700 (afternoon d20 1; d6 2): the trace twitched hard for ~30 seconds**, pressing on the hardened node in daylight hours, then fell back. A test of the new door. The node held. Tonight still 1-3. (It is not giving up.)
- **Night of day 44 (d20 18 vs 1-3): it does NOT try tonight.** Quiet.

## Day 45 (hidden; never read out)

- **Ferro's worry (set now; sound tech-lore, he says it in play):** when the ship translates out of the warp (~midday day 46) the Geller field must cycle down for a few seconds as the drive tears back into real space: the door's weakest moment. **At translation, d20: 1-6 it tries** (tonight, as before, 1-3). If it tries at translation: a real push, not a whisper; with Tamsin and Ferro at the node and the Light kitted and ready, the crew get +2 on their saves and Ferro +2 on holding the field.
- **Night of day 45 (d20 10 vs 1-3): it does NOT try.** Quiet. The translation roll at noon day 46 still stands (1-6).

## Day 46 (hidden; never read out)

- **Translation (~1200): it did NOT try (d20 18 vs 1-6).** Clean, ordinary drop (warp exit d6 4: normal). **In real space the field powers down and the trace goes dead**: whether it waits for them at the next jump is not set.
- **Gallowmere on arrival (rolled):** **orbital traffic DEAD QUIET (d6 1)**: no barges, no lifters, no tenders in the lanes, days before the harvest tithe should peak. **High orbit: nothing on the auspex (d6 1)**: whatever the old 'GALLOWMERE HIGH ORBIT' vox tags meant, nothing is showing (hidden, cold, or gone: not set). **Port control answers with a human officer (d6 5)**: **Port Controller Hesper Vael at Hearthgate, the main grain port**: brisk, polite, routine, as if nothing were odd. (Whether Vael is honest, frightened or compromised: not set; let Insight and play decide.) **~4 hours to orbit (d4 2 + 2): ~1600.**
- **Vael's official story (set now):** **eight days ago (day 38) the planetary governor (Governor Ansel Corrow) ordered a quarantine on all outbound grain: 'a blight in the outer tithe-districts'**; no barge may lift until the harvest is inspected; **the astropathic choir 'took ill' the same week** (a fever; the choir-master's word). Vael believes it, mostly (he's a port man, not a farmer). Assessors fit the story: the quarantine means the tithe is late, and Terra will want to know why. He offers a berth on Hearthgate's upper port and asks for the ship's name and the warrant code. (The truth behind the blight and the choir: not set.)
- ~1205 (hidden) Vael at the name 'Gardener Going' (d6 2): no reaction at all; he logged it like any other.
- ~1215 (hidden) Tamsin carries the Kasr Frontier openly, slung, over the clerk's coat. On an agri world a man with a rifle is ordinary; a CLERK with one is a little odd: **-1 on rolls to pass as an assessor** where an official looks closely (DM; not on ordinary crowds).
- **Day 46, the sweep (~1303-1500):** **where the Ninth lies (hidden d6 6, set now): NOT in the quarantined outer districts; inland of Hearthgate, ~300 km upriver, among the inner districts.** (The 'blight in the outer districts' story points away from it. Whether the blight is real: not set.) **Brenner's passive sweep with the ship's augur: 17 + 4 INT + 2 vox/auspex role + 2 ship's augur (DM ruling) = 25 vs hidden 20: found, and by 5 (bonus: the traffic).** What he saw: the outer districts' fields look as gold as the rest from orbit (nothing brown he can see; a blight might not show from up there). **One silo complex ~300 km upriver of Hearthgate runs warm** (grain rotting in the silos gives heat), **with a cold smear round and under it that won't hold a lock** (like the customs house's cold flickers on the Mart); **grain haulers moving on its one road: the only ground traffic he can find on this face.** It is **Silo Complex IX** (true; not known in play: no numbers can be read from orbit; Varrow or a district chart would confirm).
- **The close-up (~1530, low orbit, the ship's augur on magnify):** about a dozen tall silos on a river bend, round a rail spur and a loading yard; tarps over half the yard; haulers in and out; **a wide ring of fields cut to stubble round the whole complex (~500 m: a cleared killing ground)**; a few figures in the yard, no formations. Nothing else read yet.
- **Hearthgate upper port (set now):** the orbital dock at the head of Hearthgate's lifter threads; the ground port lies below. Grain lifts are stopped by the quarantine; a passenger car still runs. **The Valk at night:** Mira takes her out dark from the berth (a roll on the night; the upper port watches its berths).
- **~1600 at berth 7 (d6 6: the reeve's office is waiting; d6 6: Varrow himself).** **Varrow's state (hidden d6 5): HONEST and free.** Kesh's man, loyal; he knows from the logged warrant code that these 'assessors' are Kesh's people. **Watched (d6 2, 1-3 = company): a minder came with him: Under-Secretary Jarel Quint of the governor's office** ('all off-world visitors are to be accompanied during the quarantine'). Quint's nature: not set (let Insight and play decide). Varrow can't speak freely with Quint there. Quint asked about Tamsin's open rifle (the -1 applies if Tamsin rolls to pass as an assessor).
- ~1601 Tamsin's Intimidation on Quint: **14 + 2 CHA + 4 prof - 1 open rifle = 19 vs hidden 15: success.** Quint dropped the rifle and made a note on his slate (the 'corrupt government' line will reach the governor's office; what follows: not set).
- **What Varrow knows (hidden d6 3: some; set now; he says it only in private, never in front of Quint):** the outer-district reeves he trusts say their fields are clean (none of them has seen the blight); **about two weeks ago (after his last report) Silo Complex IX in the Ninth district was put under the governor's seal**, with its rail spur, and haulers run there at night despite the quarantine; he hasn't seen inside or what's behind it, and doesn't know of the 6th. **He couldn't report it: the choir went silent.** He keeps the district charts in his office at the ground port. (Whether the seal is truly Corrow's, someone in his office, or forged: not set.)
- **Quint's line (~1605): the governor expects the inspection to be done and the quarantine lifted by the Harvest Lamps** (so all the held grain would ship at once after the Lamps). Whether that's the governor's true plan: not set.
- **The Harvest Lamps on Gallowmere (set ~1615, ordinary customs):** feasts and bread at every farm gate; the reeves seal each silo with tithe-wax that night; the priests bless the first barge at dawn; the whole population out on the roads all night. **This year (Quint): Governor Corrow lights the first lamp himself at Hearthgate and speaks to every district on the vox.** (What that means: not set.)
- **QUINT'S NATURE (hidden d6 2; 1-2 the garden's, 3-4 the governor's loyal man covering for him, 5-6 an honest clerk): THE GARDEN'S MAN in the governor's office.** He minds Varrow and every off-worlder, and reports to the garden. (Whether Corrow knows, and whether Quint is behind the seal on Complex IX: not set.) He'll pass on the assessors' arrival and Tamsin's 'corrupt government' and 'hiding other things inside' lines. Whether he's seen the port log ('Gardener Going'): not set.
- ~1618 Tamsin's question (has anyone inspected the silos; the Imperium cracking down on plague areas 'hiding other things inside'): **Quint's stylus stopped for a moment at 'hiding other things inside'** (a visible tell; reading it is Insight). His line: the governor's own agri-inspectors are in the outer districts; reports go straight to the governor; 'nothing hidden on Gallowmere but grain.' Varrow: reeves are barred from the outer districts till it's done; 'fast suits me fine.'
- ~1620 **Tamsin's Perception on Quint: 17 + 5 WIS + 4 prof = 26 vs hidden 15 (a sign) / 20 (more): all of it.** Seen: (1) under a heavy scented oil, a faint sweet smell, the Mart's bloom rot; (2) pale gold dust in the welts of his polished shoes (a desk man with field dust: 'sweet dust on their boots'); (3) a thin cord at his neck under the collar seal, something small hanging out of sight under his shirt (it is a stoppered vial of bloom, like the Prophet's 'last mercy'; not seen yet). Quint didn't notice the look.
- ~1622 **Tamsin to Quint, serious: 'Thorn.' Deception 12 + 2 CHA = 14 vs hidden 15 (Quint wary after 'corrupt government' and 'hiding other things inside'): missed by 1: NEAR-MISS PARTIAL (house rule 1; first ruled a flat fail by mistake, the player caught it, fixed).** **Quint answered 'Bloom', quietly: he takes them for the garden's people, but only half** ('Not here. Not in front of the reeve.'); he'll want to verify them before he says more (with whom: not set). **Cost: Varrow heard both words.** He doesn't know the garden's codes, but he knows a challenge and answer when he hears one: **he now fears the 'assessors' are the garden's**; wary, edging away (fixable in private, with proof of Kesh).
- ~1623 Tamsin to Quint: 'Just wanted to be sure... I'll let it be for now.' Quint: 'Later, then.' His hand came off the cord. **What Quint does next (hidden d6 1; 1-3 stays at their side, 4-6 leaves to report): he stays, as their minder, to watch them till they're verified.** ~1630 at the ground port gate **he passed a folded note to a port runner** (to the garden: who are these 'assessors'? The recipient and the answer: not set). Nessa saw it (she's watched nothing but Quint since the car).
- ~1631 **Nessa slipped off after the runner (Tamsin's signal): Stealth 8 + 5 DEX + 2 infiltration = 15 vs Quint's Perception 5 + 2 = 7: unseen.** Where the runner goes and her tail: rolled when she reports.
- ~1642 **The chart: Tamsin has matched Brenner's warm complex to the Ninth district, Silo Complex IX** (river bend, rail spur, ~300 km upriver, inside the cordon-free inner districts; the cordon is far out at the edges). Varrow's chart carries no mark of the governor's seal (he keeps that in his head).
- ~1643 Varrow told Tamsin openly (in front of Quint, safe because it's official) that Complex IX is under the governor's seal and closed to reeves. Quint backed it: off limits to assessors too. (Quint now watches Tamsin's interest in IX: it sharpens what his note asks.)
- ~1645 **Tamsin to Quint, low, by the door: 'Understood. Just one thing: is the governor a "thorn" too... or is it just me and you?' Deception 19 + 2 CHA = 21 vs hidden 15: sold.** Quint trusts them more now (not fully: he still wants to know who sent them). **GOVERNOR CORROW (hidden d6 4; 1-2 the garden's, 3-4 a dupe, 5-6 honest and in the dark): A DUPE.** A frightened old man who signs what Quint puts in front of him: **the seal on Complex IX is Corrow's, put there by Quint's hand**; Corrow believes the blight is real. Quint: 'Just you and me.' Then he asked: the Mart went quiet; who sent them? **Varrow didn't hear (8 vs 15).**
- ~1646 **Tamsin: 'Not who... it's a last-resort plan... I don't have enough time to explain, but know that: it's the fallback plan.' Deception 15 + 2 CHA = 17 vs hidden 15: sold.** Quint takes them for the garden's fallback after the Mart fell ('then it's worse than we heard') and offered help: 'What do you need?' **His note to the runner is still out** (it asks who these 'assessors' are; if its answer comes back 'no such fallback', he turns: when and how, not set; Nessa is on the runner).
- ~1648 Tamsin asked Quint for the usual papers for the job, and where the rest of the 'gardeners' are. (No roll: Quint is sold, and a fallback would ask this.) **Set now:**
  - **The papers:** Quint will have Corrow sign a governor's travel pass tonight (anywhere on Gallowmere, the cordon included); at berth 7 by morning.
  - **THE CHOIR (hidden d6 1; 1-3 silenced by the garden, 4-6 truly sick): SILENCED BY THE GARDEN.** The garden has held the choir-house since the quarantine (day 38); 'no one calls out'. (How, and whether the astropaths live: not set.)
  - **WHO HOLDS THE NINTH (hidden d6 3; 1-2 Wolfer himself, 3-6 one of his officers): 'THE MAJOR'**, an officer of the 6th, not Wolfer. Name: not set.
  - **The loaders:** a garden cell at **Ostler's warehouse, Quay 4**, at Hearthgate's ground port: the dock crew who'll load the held grain at the Lamps. **It's where Quint's runner went** (the note goes upriver with the night haulers).
  - Quint wrote it on a slip of paper (in front of Varrow, he wouldn't say it): 'IX: the garden. The Major holds it. / Ostler's, Quay 4: the loaders. Ours. / The choir-house: ours since the quarantine. No one calls out.' 'Burn it when you've read it.'
- ~1651 **Quint let them take Varrow (hidden d6 5; 1-3 he comes along, 4-6 he stays):** he stays at the ground port, to see to the governor's pass and wait on his note's answer; he takes it the 'fallback' will deal with the reeve ('Terra's ears'). 
- ~1700 **Nessa's tail: 1 + 5 DEX + 2 infiltration = 8 vs hidden 15: failed; cost (hidden d6 2; 1-3 just lost him, 4-6 lost him and was noticed): she just lost him** among the barges on the river side of the terminal (toward the quays). No harm done.
- ~1801 Varrow at the hatch (hidden d6 6; 1-2 he bolts for the car, 3-6 he boards): he boarded. He believes the 'assessors' are the garden's, like Quint, and expects to die; he won't give up Kesh.
- ~1803 Tamsin showed Varrow his rosette. Varrow (honest; Kesh's man) told him what he knows (his d6 3 'some', plus what a port reeve sees): the outer-district reeves he trusts say their fields are clean, none has seen the blight; ~two weeks ago Complex IX went under the governor's seal, rail spur and all; haulers run upriver at night from the river quays despite the quarantine (whose: he doesn't know); he couldn't report: the choir went silent. **Suspicious:** Quint (his minder since the quarantine; writes down every word; the seal papers came through Quint's office); and when he went to the choir-house nine days ago with a tithe query, **men he didn't know turned him away at the gate: not temple men, not choir servants.** Nothing else.
- ~2030 **Mira's dark launch from berth 7: 5 + 6 (her stealth flying, as day 33's low run) = 11 vs hidden 15: SEEN** (missed by 4: no partial). **Who saw (hidden d6 5; 1-2 Vael hails, 3-4 logged quietly, 5-6 the garden's own eyes): the garden's eyes**: a dock watchman at berth 6 on Quint's payroll; it reaches Quint tonight. Quint takes the 'fallback' to be heading for the Ninth; what he does with it (and whether it rides upriver with the haulers to the Major): not set. Flight event d20 7: quiet.
- ~2110 **Mira's approach and landing: 19 + 6 = 25 vs hidden 15: unseen, unheard.** Set down in standing grain ~3.5 km downriver of IX, ~200 m off the river road; the team out, the Valk away low along the river back to the ship.
- ~2112 **Brenner's auspex on landing: 3 + 4 INT + 2 role = 9 vs hidden 12; Lucky reroll (mission: DM's call, this one matters; 1 of 3 today): 1 + 6 = 7: nothing useful** (the fields still holding the day's heat; a smear). **Brenner's Lucky: 2 left today.**
- ~2115 **Ground event (d20 3: something; d6 1: a hauler on the road):** a grain hauler grinding upriver toward IX on the river road, headlamps sweeping the grain ~200 m off; no warning from the auspex. Who and what's aboard: not set.
- ~2120 **Tamsin's look from the levee (3.5 km, night, pict-lens helm): Perception 18 + 5 WIS + 4 prof = 27: all that can be seen from here.** Set now:
  - **The gate:** on the river road at the stubble ring's edge: a pole barrier, a sandbagged post, **two sentries in long Guard greatcoats and rebreathers (the 6th)** with lasguns; they stopped the hauler, walked round it with a lamp, waved it in to the loading yard.
  - **Gun pits on this (downriver) side of the ring: 2 (d4 1 + 1)**, sandbagged, each with a crew-served gun's outline (heavy stubber or similar), set to cover the stubble.
  - **A searchlight on the tallest silo sweeps the stubble about every 6 minutes (d6 6).**
  - **In the loading yard under the lamps: ~90 figures (2d10 9 x 10) standing in rows, swaying, not working** (walkers; the 'beds' under the tarps likely). Brenner's auspex reads them as the cold smear.
  - **Heat shimmer rising off the silo vents** (the rot's warmth); the sweet smell on the wind.
  - **On the far side: the old granary office, windows lit, a vox mast on its roof** (the command post; the Major's, likely: not confirmed).
- ~2124 **Brenner's long scan from the levee: 16 + 4 INT + 2 role = 22 vs hidden 18: success.** **Warm bodies at IX: ~41 (4d10 21 + 20)**: ~10 in the old granary office; ~15 in long low sheds behind the silos (barracks); the gate post 2; the two gun pits ~3 each; the searchlight crew 2; hauler crews and loaders ~6 in the yard. Brenner's estimate to Tamsin: 'about forty warm'. **Cold: the ~90 in the yard, and more inside the silo bases, too many to count through the walls** (the beds). **Vox: the mast sends short coded bursts about every 15 minutes on a military band** (regimental vox discipline); not breakable from here.
- ~2128 **Sorel's route look: 12 + 3 WIS + 2 tracker = 17 vs hidden 15: found it.** The levee's river side (below the crest, out of the complex's sight) runs upriver to where the levee meets the stubble ring, ~800 m from the silos; there an old irrigation ditch (dry, waist-deep) runs off along the grain's edge. **Risks:** the nearer gun pit sits ~400 m inland of the levee's end, covering the stubble; the searchlight's sweep crosses the levee crest (stay below it); patrols on the ring's edge: none seen yet, unconfirmed (he'd want ~20 minutes of watching to be sure). ~1 hour to get there carefully.
- ~2130-2228 **The move up the levee (event d20 15: quiet). Patrol on the ring (hidden d6 2; 1-3 yes): YES, a two-man patrol** walks the ring's edge (greatcoats, rebreathers, lasguns slung, a hooded lamp); its round and whether it checks in on the 15-minute bursts: not set (Brenner could listen for it). Sorel saw them first (~150 m, coming along the dry ditch toward the levee's end).
- ~2232 **Hide at the levee's end: Tamsin 10 + 10 = 20; Nessa 25, Sorel 14, Tey 14, Brenner 13 vs the patrol's Perception (best of 2d20 13, 1) 13 + 2 - 2 (dark, lamp hooded low) = 13: all hidden** (ties stay hidden). **The patrol's path (d6 6; 4-6 turn back short): they turned back ~40 m short of the levee's end** and walked back along the ring's edge. **Their round (set now): out and back along this side of the ring, ~40 minutes a loop.**
- ~2237 **Brenner's close scan through the vents (~800 m): natural 1; Lucky reroll (mission, it matters): natural 1 again (the reroll stands): 1 + 6 = 7 vs hidden 15: nothing.** Cost (hidden d6 1; 1-3 nothing, 4-6 the rig's pulse is noticed): nothing worse; the vents' heat swamped the read. **Brenner's Lucky: 1 left today.**
- **INSIDE THE SILO BASES (set now, hidden; not known in play):** **~19 living captives (3d10 19): the 'beds'**, warm, lying in the mulch troughs, alive and being grown into walkers; **~170 more cold walkers (2d10 17 x 10)** standing in the dark of the bases. (So IX holds ~41 of the 6th outside, ~90 walkers in the yard, ~170 inside, and ~19 people still alive.)
- ~2240 **Sorel's look for a closer way: NATURAL 20 + 5 = 25.** (1) **The river margin:** past the levee's end the river runs on under a ~2 m cut bank, with a strip of mud and reeds at the waterline all the way to the complex's river side (~800 m); below the bank's lip, out of the gun pit's sight. (2) **Bonus (the 20): the searchlight has a dead arc: its operator only sweeps the landward stubble, never the river side, and the patrol never walks the bank.** (3) **At the bend, a rockcrete outfall culvert (~1.2 m across) comes out of the bank under the silos and oozes sweet green-black sludge into the river: runoff from the silo bases.** Whether it's grated, and where it leads inside: not known (set when they get there). The sludge is the rot (disease: masks on; contact risk).
- ~2242-2315 **Down the river margin (event d20 11: quiet; the dead arc and no patrol on the bank: no check).** **The outfall (hidden d6 2; 1-2 open, 3-4 a rusted grate, 5-6 a locked newer grate): OPEN**: its old grate rusted off its hinges years ago and lies in the mud. A rockcrete pipe ~1.2 m across, a hand-deep stream of sweet green-black sludge running out; a pump hums somewhere deep inside; it runs under the silos toward the bases (where it leads: set when they go in).
- ~2318 **The scan at the outfall.** **Brenner up the pipe: natural 2; LUCKY (his last today): 13 + 6 = 19 vs hidden 12: a clear read** (out of the vents' heat). **Brenner's Lucky: 0 left today.** The pipe runs ~60 m in to a pump chamber under the silo bases; **nobody in the chamber (d6 5)**. Above it in the bases: **cold, 'a hundred and fifty, maybe more'** (true ~170), **and among them ~20 warm, lying still in rows: people, alive** (true: 19 captives, the 'beds'). The Light now knows there are living people inside.
- **Tamsin's Perception 13 + 9 = 22 (Sorel 11 + 5 = 16; Nessa 2 + 3 = 5; Tey 11 + 0 = 11) vs hidden 15. Set now (d6 3: yes, someone comes down here):** steps cut into the cut bank from the outfall up to **an iron maintenance door at the base of the nearest silo** (shut; locked or not: not known); **fresh boot prints, Guard-pattern treads, two men, a day or two old (Sorel)**, and **drag marks from the steps to the water's edge: something heavy carried down between two men and put in the river.** (What: the dead the beds didn't take; let play reveal.) Nobody comes on a schedule the Light knows.
- ~2325 **Tamsin's Stealth up the pipe: 13 + 10 = 23 vs hidden 12: silent.** **The pump chamber (set now):** round rockcrete, ~6 m across, an old Munitorum pump thudding in the middle; sludge runs in through **drain grates in the ceiling: the floors of the silo bases above** (dim lamp-glow through them, shadows of feet standing, shuffling, wet breathing; and fainter, **a weak human cough** from above: one of the beds); **an iron ladder up the wall to a ceiling hatch** (into the nearest silo base); **a short service passage and stair to the iron maintenance door on the bank** (the drag marks run along it); **a rack of a dozen empty glass jars and a brass filling tap on the pump's outflow: the jars are filled here** (the Mart's jars). Nobody in the chamber.
- ~2327 **Tamsin listening at the ceiling hatch: Perception 19 + 9 = 28.** **Above it (hidden d6 2; 1-2 no tenders, 3-4 one, 5-6 two): no tenders**, only the walkers standing close round the hatch (dozens; flies) and the beds. **Heard:** shuffling, wet breathing, flies; **a woman among the beds praying in a whisper ('Emperor protect... Emperor protect...')**, a man's weak cough; far off through the silo, a door clank and a shouted order from the yard. Whether the hatch is bolted above: not known.
- **QUINT'S NOTE (set now; hidden d6 3; 1-3 'no such fallback', 4-6 no answer yet): it rides upriver on tonight's later hauler (left the quays after dark), reaching IX ~0230. The Major knows of no 'fallback': from ~0230 the garden knows the 'assessors' are false.** What follows (alert at IX, word back to Quint by the mast or the next night's run, berth 7 watched): not set yet; decide at ~0230. The Light doesn't know.
- ~2330 **The passage and stair (set now):** ~10 m of rockcrete, one caged lamp; **a two-wheeled handcart with a stained canvas sling, empty (d6 5: no body waiting)**, a pile of sacking. A stair up: **halfway, a landing with a steel inner door into the silo base (the beds' side): latched, NOT locked (d6 6)**, lamp-light under it, shuffling behind. **At the top, the iron door to the bank: barred on the inside with a heavy drop-bar, a spy-slit** (it opens from inside: a way in for the team).
- ~2334 **Tamsin at the landing door: Perception 18 + 9 = 27. Nobody coming (d6 6).** Behind the door: a walker slumped against the steel (its breathing on the metal); the beds along the wall right by the door; **two captives close enough to hear: ODILE (a woman, lucid, praying) and a hoarse man (name not set)**; chains clink when the man moves (they're chained in the troughs); a gurgle of a feed line. Odile: 'Somebody's coming for us. Somebody always comes.' The man: 'Nobody comes down here but them.'
- ~2335 Tamsin lifted the landing door's latch. **The door opens inward (into the silo base); the walker slumped against it holds it shut (d6 6: it didn't swing).** To open it he must push against the walker's weight. The click didn't stir anything.
- ~2336 **Tamsin pushed the landing door open: Athletics 9 + 3 STR + 4 prof = 16 vs hidden 15: done quietly.** The walker slid down the opening door and folded onto the floor without a crash (a man once: a spacer's coverall, a faded merchant patch, name worn off; d6 3: not the *Pilgrim's Due*). **This silo base (set now):** round, ~25 m across, caged lamps, the hopper's funnel overhead dripping sweet mash; **long troughs of black rot-mulch with 7 captives (d6 5 + 2) lying in them, chained at the ankle, feed tubes in their arms**; **~32 walkers (2d10 12 + 20) standing in rows between the troughs, swaying, heads down, dormant** (they don't react to quiet; loud noise or blood: not set); the floor hatch in the middle among them; **a far door to the yard, shut.** The other ~12 captives are in other silo bases. **Odile:** in the nearest trough, ~3 m from the door; forties, head shaved, grey with sickness, lucid; she saw him and didn't cry out: 'You came.'
- **Dormant walkers (DM rule, set ~2337):** they ignore quiet movement; a loud noise, a light in the face or fresh blood rouses those nearby (d6 per event, 1-3 they stir). Tamsin walked in quietly: none roused (one lifted its head as he passed, then let it drop).
- ~2338 **Odile's account (set now):** she and 30 others were the crew and passengers of the ***Odran's Purse*, a small grain freighter** that came in for the tithe ~two weeks ago (~day 32), before the quarantine; **taken at night at Hearthgate's upper port by men in greatcoats**, brought upriver in haulers in three lots. **31 taken; 7 left in this base; more in other silos (she hears them through the pipes: the other ~12); the rest turned** (the coverall walker at the door was Purse crew). **The lines make you sick, then quiet, then you turn.** **The tenders come at first light (~0600) and at dusk (~1900)** to change the lines and take 'whoever's stopped'. **The seven here (d6 each for the five others: 4, 2, 2, 2, 6):** Odile (lucid); **Tobin Marle**, the hoarse man (lucid; his leg swollen and going); **a third lucid but very weak (6)**; **one fevered (4)**; **three nearly turned, no longer speaking (2, 2, 2).**
- ~2340 Tamsin promised the captives he'll be back 'soon' and they'll go out the way he came. **The tenders count the troughs (chains and lines) at each change: a missing captive is found at the next change (~0600 or ~1900).** **The three nearly-turned captives (and Tobin's leg): a clock; how long before they turn: not set (decide when it matters; days, not weeks).**
- ~2341 **The way back (event d20 3; d6 5: someone at the yard door):** as Tamsin turned for the landing door (~3 m away), **the far yard door's bolt scraped: two tenders of the 6th coming in with a lamp, bringing two new captives off tonight's 2115 hauler** (bound, hooded; who they are: not set). Heard through the door: '...two more off the hauler. Put them by the Purse lot.' The door is opening now.

Overheard: 'Major wants a count by first light. Nine days, and every bed stands by the Lamps.' No alarm.

The beds stand. The bloom goes out with the grain at dawn.'** (The governor's vox address to every district is the trigger, and Quint writes what Corrow reads.) **The vox codebook survived (d20 11): Brenner can read the 6th's coded bursts.** Plus jars from the pump room's rack and the tap.
- **~0425-0505, loading (Tamsin: 'Alright, let's load everything up.'): event d20 17: quiet.** Four Valk runs up to the *Bright Light* hanging over the Ninth on her thrusters: the 3 from the third silo with Sallow's people; the 17 prisoners of the 6th (hooded, in irons, to the hold under guard; the brig takes the Commissar, the Preacher and Bell man C apart); the haul on tarps; the Light last, Tamsin on the last run. The 11 dead of the 6th and the burned silos left. **~0458, the codebook (d6 3): a burst on the 6th's band that Brenner can read: 'FOURTEEN TO NINE. NO WORD SINCE TWO-THIRTY. REPORT. REPORT.'** Brenner's direction-find off the ship's vox: **from inland, north-west, well over a hundred kilometres** (where exactly: not set; XIV). The Major doesn't yet know IX has fallen; he's waiting on a report. (Answering it, faking it, or staying silent: Tamsin's call.)
- **~0505 Tamsin's reply to Fourteen (his words, Brenner's coding: 17 + 4 INT + 2 vox = 23, the day's authenticator from the codebook): 'NINE TO FOURTEEN. HIT AT 0340, STRONG FORCE, AIR. NINE IS LOST. SURVIVORS PULLING BACK. REQUEST A MEETING POINT TO REGROUP AND DISCUSS PLANS. ADVISE.'** (New rule at the player's call: the target rolls against an ally-delivered lie.) **Fourteen's officer: 14 + 3 WIS + 2 (asking for a meeting point is odd) = 19 vs 23: FOOLED.** **Reply (d6 5: hold, we'll collect):** 'FOURTEEN TO NINE. UNDERSTOOD. HOLD WHERE YOU ARE. A HAULER COMES FOR YOU. SEND POSITION AND NUMBERS. AUTHENTICATE.' (Brenner has the next authenticator.) The hauler: from Fourteen, a driver and an escort (rolled when it comes); it would lead back to Fourteen.
- **COMPLEX XIV, 'THE HIGH FIELD' (set ~0510, hidden):** a silo complex in the Fourteenth district, **~140 km north-west of IX on the inland road, up on a plateau above the river valley** (hence 'the high field'). The Major and the first haulers went there; the last hauler (~40 walkers) is on the road to it. The ship's approach sweep (day 46) found it cold (the Major hadn't arrived); now it would read warm. Not yet known to the Light: they have the burst's bearing (north-west, 100+ km) and the inland road's line.
- **~0515 Tamsin's plan:** the position to send Fourteen: **the windbreak rise, a line of old trees on a low rise beside the inland road ~1.5 km north-west of the Ninth, looking back over the burned silos** (a sensible place for survivors to fall back to and watch). Mira drops a team there; they wait for Fourteen's hauler, **disguised as the 6th: their greatcoats and rebreathers, taken off the prisoners and the dead.** **The hauler: from Fourteen, ~140 km by road: ~2.5 h: arrives ~0800, in daylight** (not yet told: Brenner can estimate it). **The coats are soaked with the rot: each wearer rolls CON vs 13 at the end of the op, or sickened (-1) until treated; Sallow's antitoxin cures it (DM ruling; Sallow warns).**
- **~0518 The disguised team (Tamsin's pick): Tamsin, Kell, Nessa, Sorel, Morcant, Corvane** (six 'survivors'); **Isk alone on a second rise ~400 m off the far side as overwatch, fires on Tamsin's call**; Varga stays (her augmetic eye would give them away); Dace stays (Sallow's orders: his frag wound). **Brenner sent 'SIX' and the windbreak rise's position, authenticated. Fourteen's reply: 'FOURTEEN TO NINE. AUTHENTICATED. HAULER AT YOUR POSITION 0800. ON APPROACH IT SHOWS THREE SHORT. YOU ANSWER TWO LONG.'** (The lamp recognition, confirmed in the codebook.) **Hidden: the hauler (ETA 0800, d4 2): a driver, 4 escort (d4 3 + 1) and AN OFFICER (d6 2): a lieutenant of the 6th sent to debrief the survivors** (rolled when it arrives). Coats and rebreathers stripped off the prisoners in the hold.

Nessa: needle pistol and throwing knives hidden, the Accatran lascarbine slung. (Shameholder, Vigil, Last Word, Stinger, the Kasr Frontier, Officer, Mercy etc. stay aboard.)

- **~0758 THE HAULER FROM FOURTEEN (`roll_enemy traitor 6 good`, basic group, better-armed 1, guns Clean; traits d2/d36):**

I am the highest rank of the surviving few, so I've been in charge until you came along.' (Ride along into Fourteen, or spring Isk's shot here: Tamsin's call.)

The high field. Up the inland road past the Dry Fork, the old seed-station up on the plateau.'** (XIV: an old Administratum seed-station on the plateau, ~140 km; set now.)

Orders: Isk (+2) and the team's attack signal (Kell, +2).

Orders: Corvane and Kell ('the dog') at +2.** **Corvane (the commissar's chainsword, d20 + 3 STR + 2 order + 1 Kell = +6) vs the wild mastiff (+3): 14 vs 5, 19 vs 15: the mastiff DOWN.** Kell held (Tamsin on the cab). **Tamsin onto the cab step, pistol to the driver's skull: 'TURN IT OFF NOW OR YOU'RE DEAD.' Kell and Morcant dragging the mastiff wrecks for Ferro. **THE DRIVER (Will set: 75%; breaking point 25%: one of the 6th, a fanatic grunt, but he gave in at gunpoint).** What he knows (set now, for when he breaks): Fourteen's garrison (the Major, ~30 of the 6th including the ~10 who left IX with the first haulers, the beds that walked, ~100 walkers there plus the ~40 on the last hauler), the gate and the lamp signal, the seed-station's layout in outline (the main hall, the old seed vaults, the landing field), the route (the Dry Fork bridge). **Tamsin's first line ('So how many at the place...'): no leverage: it doesn't get in. No check.** He gave a safe answer.

Your lieutenant is alive, so he has better intel than you.' (Gets in: new leverage, he's the expendable one.) **Intimidation 17 + 2 CHA + 4 prof + 1 manual = 24: the 20+ band (d2 2): -10% → 65%.** Unbroken; he boasted what felt safe: thirty of the Sixth at Fourteen and 'a hundred and more' beds that walk (true as far as it goes).
- ~0808 Tamsin pushed the pistol in harder: 'And who is this Major?' (The same threat, harder: it doesn't get in. No check.) The driver: 'Grandfather's hand on Gallowmere. You'll meet him.' Nothing more. (The Major's name: not set.)
- ~0809 Tamsin: 'My team took down your operation on the Mart. I'm not scared of a lowly grain world that is a last stand for you... how do I get in the gate, and how do the haulers meet?' (Gets in: he knew the Mart fell, not that these were the ones.) **Intimidation 16 + 7 = 23: the 20+ band (d2 2): -10% → 55%.** Unbroken. **What felt safe to give:** the gate works the same as the road, three short from the gate tower, two long from the hauler; the gate party walks round the hauler with a lamp and waves it up the switchbacks to the vault yard. (True. Not given: the garrison's layout, who meets the hauler, the Major.)

Tamsin's ~0810 line (are you really one of them; the Mart's down, Wolfer's going down, a last stand; alive or dead?) gets in (Wolfer named, the regiment's end): Intimidation will check pending.
- ~0810 Tamsin: 'Let me ask you something that will decide your fate. Are you really one of them... or are you in it for another reason? This is it for the Sixth: the Mart is down, Wolfer is going down, and this is a last stand for the Sixth... after I take this out, it's over. So do you want to be alive or dead?' **Intimidation 11 + 7 = 18: the 15-19 band (d3 3): -8% → 47%.** Unbroken (25%). He gave what felt safe: **the Major's name: MAJOR ULRIC MORDANE (set now)**, and defiance for the regiment: Wolfer won't fall to them. (Still holding: who meets the hauler, Fourteen's layout and garrison detail.)
- **The driver's age (set ~0811, d6 1): about 19.** A hive kid from Tertium on Atoma Prime who joined the Sixth as a replacement at fifteen, after it turned: the regiment is the only family he's had (fits 'follows the regiment'). Visible: when his mask slipped, a young face, sores just starting at the jaw.
- ~0812 Tamsin: 'You're just a kid, son... you don't know how this ends. I won't believe you actually want a god to poison your own life. You're here because you've only had one family... this regiment... The Inquisition is here, if you even know what that is, and we're here to stop this.' (Gets in deep: his true reason, and the Inquisition.) **Persuasion NATURAL 20 + 2 CHA + 1 manual = 23: the 20+ band (d2 1: 9%), doubled by the natural 20: -18% → 29%. Breaking point 25%: NOT YET BROKEN (on the edge).** He gave his name, **LENK**, cried, and asked what happens to him if he talks.
- ~0813 Tamsin: 'I have men on my team that shot at me. I understand being on the edge and having nowhere to turn. Others won't feel the same for you, so tell me, son, tell me everything you know... and you can figure out what happens after.' (Gets in: a real door.) **Persuasion 8 + 3 = 11: the 10-14 band (d3 2: 4%) → 25%. Breaking point 25%: not BELOW it: still unbroken, on the very edge** (any line that gets in breaks him). What holds him: fear of Major Mordane ('he always knows').
- ~0814 Tamsin: 'Your Grandfather cannot help you anymore. You have to resent... you have to turn away. The Major will have a shot through his head. My team has taken out Thask, the Sixth taking over a Mart, a traitor lord... one Major is nothing to my team.' (Gets in: it answers his fear of Mordane.) **Persuasion 8 + 3 = 11: 10-14 band (d3 3: 5%) → 20%: BELOW 25%: LENK IS BROKEN.** He answers truthfully all he knows (set now):
  - **Fourteen, 'the high field':** an old Administratum seed-station on a plateau, ~140 km up the inland road; **over the Dry Fork bridge**, then **switchbacks up the escarpment** to a **walled compound**. **The gate tower** at the top of the switchbacks (3 of the Sixth on the gate; the lamp drill: three short, two long; they walk round the hauler with a lamp, then wave it on).
  - **Inside:** **the vault yard** (where haulers unload) in front of **the seed vaults**, deep stone vaults cut into the plateau: **the walking beds are kept in the vaults** (~100 there, ~40 more coming on the last hauler); **the main hall** (the old station hall: **the Major's command, the vox mast on its roof**); **the old dormitories** (barracks); **the threshing barns**; **a landing field** behind the hall (an old cargo pad; nothing on it but a broken lifter).
  - **Garrison: ~30 of the Sixth:** 3 on the gate; ~6 in the vault yard with a **heavy stubber on the hall roof** over the yard; ~10 in the hall with the Major (and his sergeant, **Sergeant Kreel**, and a plague-priest he calls **'the Gardener's voice'**); 2 pairs walking the wall; the rest asleep in the dormitories by day.
  - **Who meets the hauler:** Sergeant Kreel and the yard party; **the Major comes down into the vault yard himself to hear survivors' reports** (he did when the first haulers came in from the Ninth).
  - **The Lamps:** on the first night of the Lamps, when the Governor speaks to every district on the vox, the Major sends the beds down the roads to the barge ports and the districts; the grain lifts at dawn. **The word itself: he doesn't know it** (only the Major, the plague-priest and 'the man in the Governor's house', i.e. Quint).
  - **About himself:** Lenk, 19, from Tertium on Atoma Prime; joined the Sixth at fifteen as a replacement after it turned; never wanted the god, only the regiment. He'll do what he's told now.

- **THE MAIN HALL, GROUND FLOOR (set now, as seen from the door):** the old seed-station's grading hall: long, stone-flagged, high dusty windows; sorting tables and grading sieves shoved to the walls; the 6th's command post in the middle: **a war-table with a big map of the districts pinned flat, red lines drawn on it** (detail when looked at); cots along the left wall; a kettle on a heater, still warm; **the regiment's colours hung at the far end, defaced** (the 6th's eagle cut away, the three circles daubed over it); flies, the sweet rot; **a stair up to the first floor (the Major's rooms)**; an iron ladder in the corner up through a hatch to the roof (the vox room and the stubber parapet); **a back door to the landing field**. Empty of the living (all 30 of the 6th accounted for, as Lenk said).
- **THE MAIN HALL, GROUND FLOOR: what's there (set ~1138, before Tamsin's search roll; hidden):** **in plain sight (no roll):** the war-table map (Gallowmere's districts; red lines from Fourteen and the other complexes down the roads to the barge ports, a number on each road, IX crossed through). **Search, Perception d20 + 9 vs 12:** the 6th's kit bags under the cots: personal effects, **letters home to Atoma Prime never sent**, a little coin (d20 x 5). **vs 15:** **behind the defaced colours, a niche in the stone: an EMPTY RELIC CASE shaped for a pistol, its brass plate reading that the piece was presented to the Moebian 6th by the forge for the regiment's defence of a hive, 'held in trust by the regimental Enginseer'** (the volkite's story: the 6th's honour relic; the Enginseer, loyal, killed when the 6th turned; dormitory man 7 took it off his body: Ferro can confirm from the gun and the man).
- **~1138 Tamsin searched the hall's ground floor: Perception 18 + 9 = 27: beats 12 and 15: both found.** **The kit bags: a bundle of letters home to Atoma Prime, never sent (the early ones homesick and loyal, the late ones full of 'Grandfather's love'), and 90 Thrones (d20 18 x 5).** **Behind the defaced colours, the niche: the EMPTY RELIC CASE (worn velvet in the shape of a pistol; a dark stain on the lid's edge); the brass plate: 'PRESENTED TO THE MOEBIAN SIXTH BY THE FORGE OF RYZA FOR THE HOLDING OF THE KALT GATE. HELD IN TRUST BY THE REGIMENTAL ENGINSEER. LET IT BURN THE EMPEROR'S FOES.'** (Set now: the forge and the battle.)
- **~1140 Tamsin took the letters, the relic case and the 90 Thrones, and read the war-table (no roll). THE BEDS GO AT THE WORD. DAWN: THE GRAIN LIFTS.'**; in the margin: **'Word by the Governor's address. Q confirms.'** (Q: Quint, aboard.) No word written anywhere.
- **~1141 Tamsin rolled up the war-table map (taken) and went up the stair to the Major's rooms to search.** **THE MAJOR'S ROOMS (set now, before the roll; hidden):** **in plain sight:** an officer's room under the eaves, a camp cot made tight, a desk under the window looking down on the vault yard, the Major's greatcoat on a hook, a shelf of the station's old seed ledgers, a small daubed shrine (the three circles, a jar of flies), **a heavy footlocker, locked** (opening it: a key not found on him; forced with a STR roll or cut); **the duty log open on the desk** (the hauler runs; the last entries this morning: 'Nine silent'; 'Nine reports hit, survivors; hauler sent'; 'Drayle not returned'). **Search, Perception d20 + 9 vs 12:** in the desk drawer, **a packet of coded slips signed Q** (the 6th's code: Brenner can read them with the IX codebook). **vs 17:** under a loose board beneath the cot, **the regiment's pay strongbox** (coin: d6 x 500 when opened). **In the footlocker (when opened):** **a padded cold-case of SIX SEALED JARS of the culture, each labelled with a port: LOWMERE, SALTBEND, KETTLE FORD, VARN QUAY, and two HEARTHGATE** (the quiet bloom for the grain: to go down the roads with the beds at the word, for the loaders at each port), plus the Major's commission and a Moebian service medal.
