Morcant (recoil 1): 19: down; 22: down: NONE STANDING.** The rest had nothing left to shoot. **THE LEFT VAULT IS FINISHED: all 44 down for good.** **Middle 48 + left 44 = 92 down.** **The right vault: locked, ~45, pounding.** **Frags left: the first ring's middle and right arcs (~4), the second ring (~5); Six Bells 4/6; the satchel still on the middle door's lintel.** The Light all Fine.
- **~1246 Tamsin walked to the right vault and listened (the player's narration taken as what he hears: less pounding than the others, and deep inside a low, deep slosh and a growl: 'something else is in there'; the player's OOC nudge: a boss fight).** **HIDDEN (rolled now): THE RIGHT VAULT holds 24 walkers (d6 4 + 20; fewer than the others: the thing in there has torn and fed on the rest) and A PLAGUE OGRYN OF THE 6TH (d6 1 on a DM table: 1-4 a plague Ogryn, 5-6 a spawn).** **The Plague Ogryn (roles.md, Ogryn; cultist side note: the god's beast, chained, starved, set loose):** ~3 m, bloated with the rot, belly split and sloshing; **STR 68 (+6)**, DEX +1; **health: Fine, Grazed, Bruised, Hurt, Badly hurt, Down** (three extra stages); **shots at it +2 (a big target)**; no armour, but its bulk; **fists and the length of its own chain**; **once a fight, a CHARGE** (a hit knocks the target flat and anyone right behind; a DEX dodge to get clear); **berserk: its boss (the Major) is down and gone: it charges the nearest enemy and ignores everything**. **CHAINED (d6 3)** by a collar to a ring at the back of the vault (~10 m of chain: it can't reach the doorway while the chain holds; **each beat it strains: STR d20 + 6 vs 18 to tear the ring out of the rock**). **When it goes Down, its belly bursts: everyone within ~3 m, CON save vs 13 or the rot (sickened)** (DM design, set now). Its name, if it has one: not set.
- **THE RIGHT VAULT, BEAT 1 (~1247). (The player narrated Tamsin's part and the chain breaking, and handed the rest to the DM: 'what happens after is up to you'.)** Tamsin: 'The hell?' He unlocked the right door and spun the wheel; the door swung; **the 24 walkers were clustered at the back round the Ogryn, not pressed at the door (why the pounding was thin)**; **the chain broke (the player's narration, taken as canon: the ring tore out of the rock)**; Tamsin froze a moment (no dodge) and **THE PLAGUE OGRYN CHARGED (its once-a-fight charge, spent): knocked him flying, d6 3 + 3 = 6 m, flat on his back in the dirt ~8 m from the door (2 m short of the first ring's right arc: if those wires go, he's inside the blast).** **The heavy hit: the refractor field, d6 5: THE FIELD TOOK IT (no health step; the field flared).** Vigil on its sling, Shameholder on his back. **The first surge (d6 1 + 8): 9 walkers out (0-5 m), shambling after the Ogryn toward Tamsin** (in contact next beat). 15 still inside. **The Ogryn stands over Tamsin, berserk** (its boss gone: it goes for the nearest enemy). **DM design for the boss (set now, hidden till seen in play): its bloat soaks plain hits: only SOLID hits (beat by 3+), crits, fire and blasts take a stage off it** (like the walkers' resilience); shots at it +2 (big target). **The Light held (waiting on Tamsin's mark) as it happened in the 6th's part of the beat; they act next beat.**
- **THE RIGHT VAULT, BEAT 2 (~1248).** Tamsin: **'LIGHT, DESTROY THAT MONSTROSITY.'** (The player confirmed: he meant ALL LIGHT: the rifles, Six Bells, Teo and Mira.) No named orders: no +2. Tamsin flipped onto his feet, jumped back and broke away toward the team (cutting across to Tavi's chalk lane), firing Vigil at the Ogryn.
  - **Breaking off: the Ogryn's free swing (d20 + 6 STR): 8 + 6 = 14 vs Tamsin's dodge (+11): 13 + 11 = 24: 10 higher: EVADED.** The fist went past his head; he was away.
  - **Vigil on the run at the Ogryn (+11, +2 big target = +13; ~20 m on average: 10; the boss counts only solid hits, crits, fire and blasts): 8 → 21: solid: the Ogryn GRAZED; 18: a VIGIL CRIT: 33: two stages: BRUISED, then HURT.** **Vigil heat 3: Korren's breath: it must vent; firing anyway at 3 heat: d20, 1-5 cracks the coil.** **The last two dice (19, 2): held: asked of the player** (fire anyway, or stop). Vigil 20/30.
  - **Tamsin's call: Vigil slung to vent (19 and 2 not fired; heat clears after this beat); he planted his feet in the chalk lane ~22 m from the right door (past both rings, ~14 m from the Ogryn) and drew Shameholder. Turn ended.**
  - **ALL LIGHT on the Ogryn (Hurt; shots at it +2; it counts only solid hits, crits, fire and blasts):** **Hollis (Six Bells, +8, the spot by the Ogryn, hidden 15): 14 + 8 = 22: the frag burst at its feet: BLAST: the Ogryn BADLY HURT; 2 walkers behind it caught (d6 2): DOWN.** Drum 3/6. **Teo (turret, +6 + 2 big target = +8 vs 15): natural 1: miss; 10 → 17: a hit, NOT solid: it sloshes into the bloat, no effect; 13 → 23 (+2): SOLID: THE PLAGUE OGRYN DOWN.** **Its belly burst** (within ~3 m: none of the Light; Tamsin ~14 m off: clear). (Survival d6 after the fight, as for any enemy Down.)
  - **Leftovers onto the walkers (vs 15 roof, 17 wall; walkers +0):** **Mira (+4, recoil 1): 12, 2: miss; 19: down. Kell: 23: down; 12: miss; 18: down. Morcant: 24: down; 20: down; 11: miss. Tey: 28: down; 15: not solid (may rise); natural 1** (the nine out all down). **Into the vault's mouth (15 inside):** **Ferro: 20: down; 17: not solid; 8: miss. Isk: 22: down; 15, 16: miss. Sorel: 15 miss; 17: not solid; natural 1. Corvane: 12, 11, 15: miss. Varga: 18: not solid; 25: down; 29: down. Tavi: 21: down; 19: not solid; 22: down.**

  - **After beat 2:** **THE PLAGUE OGRYN: DOWN** (belly burst). **The right vault's 24: 14 down for good; 5 down not solid (rise on a d6 6 next beat); 5 up, just out (0-5 m).** **Tamsin: Fine, Shameholder out, Vigil venting (heat 0 next beat), 20/30; Dace beside him.** The Light all Fine. Six Bells 3/6.
- **THE RIGHT VAULT, BEAT 3 (~1249).** Rise checks for beat 2's five not-solid downs (d6): 1, 4, 5, 3, 5: none. Tamsin asked about charging the downed Ogryn to take its head; the DM laid out the risk (the 5 walkers beside it, its burst gut: a CON save, and it can't rise mid-fight); **Tamsin held his ground: 'FIRE ON THOSE, DON'T LET THEM GET UP.'** (The all-Light release from beat 2 stands: everyone fires.)
  - **Hollis (Six Bells +8 vs 15): 4 → 12: missed by 3: no near-miss: the frag burst high on the rock face above the right door, harmless.** Drum 2/6. **Teo: 12, 13, 12: miss. Mira: 12, 5, 12: miss.**
  - **Kell: 24: down; 12: miss; 21: down. Morcant: 16: not solid (may rise); 10, 13: miss. Tey: 14: miss; 22: down; 20: down: the last five all down.**

Vigil 20/30 (heat 0 after venting). **Still owed at the end of the op: the rot-coat CON saves for the six who wore the 6th's coats (vs 13; Sallow's antitoxin).**
- **~1250 Tamsin: 'Coats off, people. Burn everything.'** (Kell: 'Coats off! Into a pile!') The Sixth's greatcoats and cracked masks stripped off and heaped on the burned earth. **THE ROT-COAT SAVES (CON vs 13; the coats worn since ~0530; Isk too, the seventh 'survivor' since ~0832: DM ruling, he passed the gate in one):** **Kell 11 + 3 = 14: clean. Nessa 5 + 1 = 6: SICKENED. Sorel 17 + 2 = 19: clean. Morcant 18 + 3 = 21: clean. Corvane 17 + 3 = 20: clean. Isk 3 + 2 = 5: SICKENED.** (Sickened: -1 on all rolls until treated; Sallow's antitoxin aboard cures it.) **Tamsin's save (d20 + 2 CON + 4 proficiency (a Fighter's CON saves) = +6): the player's 11 + 6 = 17: CLEAN.** **Tavi first disarms and recovers his unspent frags (~8) and the satchel** before anything is lit. **'Burn everything': the coats, the dead (116 walkers, the Ogryn, the 6th's 13 dead), the vaults: clear. Kell's question (in voice): the barns' tithe grain too (not checked for the rot), and the buildings?** Asked of the player.
- **~1251 Tamsin: 'Yes, all of it. Everything is contaminated.'** (Kell: 'You heard him! Everything burns!') **The plan in hand: Tavi recovers his ~8 frags and the satchel (no roll: his own rig); the Light roll the barns' promethium drums out and douse the coat pile, the heaps of dead, the Ogryn, the three vaults' mouths, the dormitories and the main hall; the barns themselves (grain and the rest of the fuel) go up last; the two haulers too.** **Brenner, from the ship, as they start (in voice): the vox room on the hall roof hasn't been searched: the 6th's vox set and its logs would burn with it.** Asked of the player: search the vox room first, or burn it with the rest.
- **~1252-1318 Tamsin: 'Shit... hold it, team. We need to search this entire area, then let's do it.'** (Kell: 'Hold the torches! Turn it all over first!') **Everything not yet searched, set and rolled now:** **the VOX ROOM on the hall roof (Ferro and Sorel):** the 6th's vox set (a field set wired to the mast) and **its log: a spool-slate of the traffic in the 6th's cipher** (for Brenner and the codebook). **Hidden, what it holds (d6 2: local traffic only):** Fourteen's bursts to and from IX, the haulers, the barge-port cells, and Q's channel at Hearthgate; nothing off-world. **THE THREE VAULTS (Kell, Morcant, Hollis, Tey):** bones and filth; **the Ogryn's torn-out ring and chain** at the back of the right vault; **a heap of belongings stripped from the people made into beds** (clothes, boots, travel papers, ship's ID discs: crews and passengers of passing ships, among them discs from the ***Odran's Purse***, Odile's ship): evidence for Kesh; **60 Thrones** in loose coin (d20 6 x 10); looting d20 8: nothing rare. **The Ninth's hauler: empty. The gate tower (d6 1): nothing. The wall and the landing field: nothing new.** **~1318: everything doused and the Light at the gate, ready to burn; who lights it: asked of the player.**
- **~1320 Tamsin: 'Tavi... you want the honours, for your beautiful masterpiece of a trap?'** (Tavi: 'Boss... it'd be an honour.') **Tavi clamped his demo satchel to the fuel drums at the back of the barns, ran back to the gate (~95 m: inside the detonator's ~100 m) and blew it (no roll: calm, his own charge).** The barns went up in a fireball; the fire ran down the promethium through the yard: the coats, the dead, the Ogryn, the vaults, the dormitories, the hall, the haulers. **FOURTEEN BURNS (~1321).** The satchel spent; Tavi keeps his ~8 recovered frags (and the scrap-packed breaching charge). The Light at the gate, all Fine (Nessa and Isk sickened, for Sallow).
- **~1322-1335 Tamsin: 'Mira, get us out of here.'** (Mira: 'Coming down. Two minutes.') To Nessa and Isk: 'Keep your distance... you guys already smell worse than you did.' (Nessa: 'Says the man who's been wearing a dead traitor's coat since dawn, big brother.' Isk: '...Fair.') **The Valk put down on the road outside the gate; the Light boarded; up to the *Bright Light* (~1330).** **Aboard: Sallow took Nessa and Isk straight to the medicae: her antitoxin: CURED (as set: the antitoxin cures the coat rot; no roll).** Brenner met the Valk at the ramp for **the vox spool and Q's coded slips** (decoding takes time: results when Tamsin asks). **Behind them, Fourteen burning on the plateau.** The Light all Fine. **~1335: aboard the *Bright Light*, over the burning high field.**
- **~1340 THE INTERROGATIONS BEGIN: QUINT FIRST (the player's pick).** (Before it, the DM and player rewrote the difficulty rules: codex Part 8 Enemy numbers and tactics, bestiary bosses.md, vehicles.md, new roles and the 6th's team units; boss meter 1%.)
- **QUINT, HIDDEN (set now):** **Traits (d2/d36): Iron will (good 27: +1 against interrogation, so Tamsin's will checks on him take -1; his Will starts 10% higher); Slow reflexes (bad 29, n/a).** **Will 80%** (70: a believer, but a desk man who's watched his side fall: taken in his own study, the choir-house and Quay 4 taken, and **he saw the Major walked past his cell in irons ~1330**; +10 Iron will). **Breaking point 25%.** (He carried a bloom vial as his 'last mercy' and was going for it when taken.)
  - **What he knows (true; he gives it only when broken):** **THE WORD: 'THE SEED IS GOOD'**, a line he wrote into the Governor's Lamps address (Corrow reads it, innocent, on the first night of the Lamps over the district vox; the cells move on it). **The port cells:** Hearthgate, Ostler's at Quay 4 (taken); **Lowmere: the Corn Exchange's night crew, foreman Abel Garrow; Saltbend: the salt-barge dockmaster, Hesk, and his lightermen; Kettle Ford: the ferry family, the Dunns (father and three sons), who work the grain punts; Varn Quay: the tithe-weighers' house, under the chief weigher, Orla Fenn.** Each cell was to collect its jar from the Major's runners on the Lamps' eve and dose the grain as it loaded. **The grain:** about **a fifth of the held stock is already seeded** (IX's own silos' grain, shipped down to **Hearthgate's Silo Row 3** before the quarantine); the rest is clean, to be dosed at loading. **The Governor:** Corrow is a dupe; Quint writes his addresses and his seals; Corrow sealed IX on Quint's word ('a blight'). **Above him:** the Major and the Gardener's voice (the priest); the ships that came for the grain came through the Mart (Thask's, fallen); **'the Captain'** (Wolfer) only by name and the green seal, never met. **He doesn't know** where Wolfer is or anything off-world beyond that.
- **~1342 Tamsin took Quint out of the cells to a secluded room, so no other prisoner hears what's said and leverage can be used again on the next one (the player's call). FIXED at the player's call: the ship has no 'purser's strongroom' and no row of six brig cells (the ship map: two cells, CELL 1 and CELL 2, on the lower deck under the hold, beside the forge, the las room and Ferro's tech workshop).** **The important prisoners are in Cells 1 and 2, four to a cell (the Major, the priest, Kreel, Quint; Vosk, Drayle, IX's preacher, Bell man C); the rest hooded and in irons in the hold under guard.** **THE QUIET ROOM: the SPARE CABIN on the upper deck** (between Varga's cabin and the airlock side, across from the bunk room; the player's pick): a narrow cabin with a bolted cot frame, a fold-down desk, a stool and a locker; a door that shuts; Quint's irons chained to the cot frame. Dace outside the door. (The interrogation room from here on.)
- **~1343 QUINT, line 1.** Tamsin: 'We found your slips, Q... so where's the rest of the places on the map? Tell me where, and what's left on here.' **Gets in (new leverage: he didn't know his slips were found, or that the Light had been through the Major's own rooms at Fourteen).** **(Rule confirmed by the player: the DM rolls will checks openly, the subject's reaction; written into the codex.)** **Intimidation 3 + 7 - 1 (Iron will) = 9: the 9-or-less band (d2 2): -2% -> 78%.** Unbroken. He gave what felt safe: the slips are his (no use denying a hand they've got); the ports are on the Major's map, five ports, five numbers; 'what's at them is grain, and the men who load it, as at every port on every agri world'; he writes letters, he never went upriver.
- **~1345 QUINT, line 2.** Tamsin: 'We took your Major and burned IX. That's it, your fight is over... we clear out these last few people and leave this place. But here's the thing: my people like it when others cooperate, so if you do, you might live for it... What's the word? The trigger in the Governor's Lamps.' **Gets in (new: IX burned: he was taken at ~0200, before the assault; he didn't know). Intimidation (the offer of his life with death the other side of it) 6 + 7 - 1 = 12: the 10-14 band (d3 1): -3% -> 75%.** Unbroken. Safe answers: IX burning shook him (a long silence); then: 'Then you've done the Governor's work for him: the blight's burned out, let the Lamps go on'; **the word: denied ('there is no word; there's a speech, about bread')**; and on living: 'Live? For what? In one of your cells until your Inquisitor wants me burned in a square?' (He's thinking about it; holding.)
- **~1347 QUINT, line 3.** Tamsin: 'Talk and you won't burn... you'll be able to have a second chance at life, rejoice your world. You're not too far gone, kid. You're saveable, unlike Fourteen: it's gone too, and your hundred and forty beds are... gone.' **Gets in (new: Fourteen burned and the beds gone; and it answers the fear he just said aloud). Persuasion (the offer of a second chance) NATURAL 20 + 3 - 1 = 22: the 20+ band (d2 1: 9%), DOUBLED by the natural 20: -18% -> 57%.** Unbroken, but cracked wide. He gave what felt safe and true: **the Governor is no gardener: 'Corrow is a fool, not one of us. He reads what I write and seals what I give him. He sealed IX because I told him it was blighted. Leave him out of it.'**; **'There is a word.'** (admitted; not given); and he asked for terms: 'What does a second chance mean, from an Inquisitor? Say it plainly.'
- **~1348 QUINT, line 4.** Tamsin: 'Why do you care about the Governor? Tell me and I'll answer the question. That's not a hard one, it's just a simple one.' **Gets in (it touches what he guards). Set now (hidden d6 6; 1-2 his patron since boyhood, 3-4 guilt at ruining a decent man, 5-6 family): GOVERNOR CORROW IS QUINT'S BROTHER-IN-LAW: married to his sister ELIN; two children (a boy of ten, a girl of seven) who call Quint uncle; they live in the Governor's house in Hearthgate's upper town. None of them is the garden's.** **Persuasion 5 + 3 - 1 = 7: the 9-or-less band (d2 2): -2% -> 55%.** Unbroken. He answered (true; he gave it to buy their safety): 'Because he married my sister. Because their children call me uncle. Because none of them has ever done anything but bake bread for the Lamps.' He wants Tamsin's answer on terms now. **New leverage for Tamsin: his sister and her children.**
- **~1350 QUINT, line 5.** Tamsin: 'Some of my men were traitors once... but not any more. They fight for our cause. And if you prove yourself back to the Inquisition, you might be able to leave, or stay and go back to your people... but that's only if you cooperate.' **Gets in (new: the Light takes turncoats back, and there are living proofs). Persuasion 19 + 3 - 1 = 21: the 20+ band (d2 2): -10% -> 45%.** Unbroken, moving. He gave his terms and something that felt safe: **condition: 'Elin and the children are left alone, whatever happens to me, and Corrow keeps his chair. Swear it.'**; and, believing the jars burned at Fourteen: **'Without the beds the word moves nothing but the loaders. And the loaders move nothing without what the Major sends them. Unless you burned that too.'** (True: the cells depend on the jars; he doesn't know the Light took all six.)
- **~1352 QUINT, line 6.** At 'my price' Tamsin laughed: 'You think just because I give you some nice words I'll negotiate with you? Listen: you're the least of the people I need information from. I just felt like starting with you was right. There are four others with more information... so you play by my rules, you hear?' **Gets in (new: his value is not unique; the others in the cells know the word too, which is half true: the Major and the priest do). Intimidation 8 + 7 - 1 = 14: the 10-14 band (d3 1): -3% -> 42%.** Unbroken; the haughtiness gone. Safe and true: 'The Major will die before he gives you a syllable, and the priest... the priest wants to burn. He'll sing at you while you do it.' Then: 'Your rules, then. Ask.' (He dropped the demand for an oath; the sister and the children are still what he's protecting.)
- **~1353 QUINT, line 7 (after a direct 'What's the word, and who runs the other four ports?' that didn't get in: the same card, nothing new; he asked what happens to Elin and the children).** Tamsin: 'Talk and we'll discuss the matter of your deal. You cooperate and I can put that into a report that makes your life easier... you understand?' **Gets in, barely (a vague promise that touches his deal). Persuasion 2 + 3 - 1 = 4: the 9-or-less band (d2 1): -1% -> 41%.** Unbroken. Unmoved: 'A report. You'd write me a kind line in a report.' He wants their names kept out of it, said plainly: 'A report won't stand between my sister and a purge. Say Elin and her children are kept out of it. Say it, and mean it.'
- **~1354 QUINT, line 8.** Tamsin: 'A report got me most of my team. A report did more than any of you cultists. A report saves fucking lives.' In his face: 'A fucking report can save your family, and you know it. So you have one more shot, or I'm moving on.' **Gets in (a plain answer to 'say it': the report protects the family; and the ultimatum: his last chance). Intimidation 9 + 7 - 1 = 15: the 15-19 band (d3 2): -7% -> 34%.** Unbroken, nearly. **A down payment, true, chosen to protect his own (his sister bakes the Lamps bread): 'Silo Row 3. At Hearthgate's ground port. A fifth of the held grain: it came down from IX before the seal. It's already seeded. Don't let it go to the bakers.'** (The word and the names still held.)
- **~1355 QUINT, line 9.** Tamsin: 'The word, kid... and the port cells. Last chance, or you'll never see those kids a day in your life again.' **Gets in (new: the threat is to him and the children: separation for life). Intimidation 5 + 7 - 1 = 11: the 10-14 band (d3 2): -4% -> 30%.** Unbroken, by a hair. He gave one cell, to buy time, and kept the word as his last card: **'Lowmere. The Corn Exchange. The night crew, under a foreman called Abel Garrow.'** 'The rest...' He couldn't make himself. 'The word is all I have left to buy them with.'
- **~1356 QUINT, line 10.** Tamsin: 'Then you know what it means. You hand it over and you get a second chance... and another way to see your family.' **Gets in (new: the word as his way back to them, the carrot after the stick). Persuasion 4 + 3 - 1 = 6: the 9-or-less band (d2 1): -1% -> 29%.** Unbroken, on the edge. Another cell for a promise: **'Varn Quay. The tithe-weighers' house. The chief weigher, Orla Fenn.'** 'That's two. Two names for a promise.' The word and Saltbend and Kettle Ford still held.
- **~1357 QUINT, line 11.** Tamsin: 'You keep prolonging this. I won't promise you unless you give it all up. That's it: after this, if you don't give it up, I'll move on and you'll never see anything again.' **Gets in (new: the promise he asked for, offered for everything; the ultimatum itself is the third time and wouldn't have alone). Intimidation 2 + 7 - 1 = 8: the 9-or-less band (d2 2): -2% -> 27%.** Unbroken, by 2%. He gave the last two cells and held only the word, for the oath: **'Saltbend: the salt-barge dockmaster, Hesk, and his lightermen. Kettle Ford: the ferry family, the Dunns. The father and his three sons. They work the grain punts.'** 'That's all of them. Every name. The word... the word I'll give you when I hear you swear it. On whatever an Inquisitor swears on.'
- **~1358 QUINT BREAKS.** Tamsin turned and walked for the door without a word (a bluff, played straight: he'd have gone). **Gets in (new: he walks; the oath refused). Deception 13 + 3 - 1 = 15: the 15-19 band (d3 2): -7% -> 20%: BELOW HIS BREAKING POINT (25): BROKEN.** Before Tamsin's hand reached the door: **'THE SEED IS GOOD.'** Then all of it, true (what he knows): **the word 'THE SEED IS GOOD' is a line in the Governor's Lamps address** (Quint wrote it into the speech; Corrow reads it, innocent, on the first night of the Lamps over the district vox; at it the Major's runners carry each cell its jar, and the beds go down the roads); **the cells collect their jars from the Major's runners on the Lamps' eve and dose the grain as it loads at dawn**; **Silo Row 3 at Hearthgate: the seeded fifth from IX**; the rest of the held grain clean; **Corrow a dupe, sealed IX on Quint's word; Elin and the children innocent**; above Quint: the Major and the Gardener's voice; the grain ships came through the Mart (Thask's); **'the Captain' (Wolfer) known to him only by name and the green seal, never met, nothing off-world beyond that**; the bloom vial his 'last mercy'. **Broken: he answers truthfully what he knows from here on.** (No oath given by Tamsin.)
- **~1400 Tamsin to Quint: 'Good choice. Your family will be fine. You will see.'** (Tamsin's word to Quint, given after the break: **Elin, the children and Corrow to be kept safe**; a promise on the record, for Kesh's report.) Quint back to Cell 1. **Who's next: awake: Major Mordane (Hurt, patched); Down and out until Sallow patches them (Down to Hurt, a stimm each; d20 + 4 WIS + 4 + 1 Kell vs 15): the plague-priest, Kreel, Commissar Vosk (IX), Lieutenant Drayle (the windbreak)**; also aboard: Lenk (broken), the Hound man, IX's preacher and Bell man C, and the rest in the hold.
- **~1405 MAJOR ULRIC MORDANE, the second interrogation (the spare cabin). HIDDEN (set now):** **Traits (rolled at Fourteen): Iron will (Tamsin's will checks -1; Will +10%); Forgotten Penitent (n/a).** **Will 95%** (85: a proud officer and a believer, captured and his compound burned; +10 Iron will). **Breaking point 15%** (near-fanatic: he'll take a great deal). Hurt (Sallow's patch), in pain; in irons.
  - **What he guards and feels (the levers, hidden):** **pride in the old regiment** (he kept his commission and his Moebian service medal in the footlocker; the Kalt Gate); **his men** (the unsent letters home were from his own companies); **resentment of Wolfer**: at the turning the Captain took the regiment over a Major's head, and Mordane has served under a junior officer ever since; **the purge: the red page 'STRUCK. BY ORDER OF THE CAPTAIN.' is in Mordane's own hand: he struck the names, and he shot Enginseer Hale himself** (the volkite was Hale's charge; the stain on the case is Hale's); faith in the Grandfather (not a lever: it holds him up).
  - **What he knows (true; given only when broken):** the word (Quint has given it); **WOLFER is on Atoma Prime, in Hive Tertium, holding it with the cult** (with the Carnak twins); **Gallowmere was Wolfer's 'garden'**: grow the quiet bloom in Maren's blood-culture, seed the tithe grain, ship it through the Mart to the sub-sector's hive worlds; **the ships: the free trader *SWEET ABUNDANCE*, master HOLLAN DREX, carried the grain out and the 'beds' in, through the Mart and Thask** (set now: Kesh's 'someone is feeding them ships'); where Drex is now: **due back at Gallowmere for the Lamps grain** (~day 55-56); the purge (he did it, on Wolfer's order); the regiment's numbers here (~210 on the roll; ~60 at IX and XIV, the rest dead, turned to beds, or with the haulers and the port runners).
- **~1406 THE MAJOR, line 1:** 'That was me... good shooting. If you were on the right team you could've had men like me... men who fight for the right.' **Didn't get in** (a boast, nothing new). He answered with pride he let show: a thousand men, **the Kalt Gate held forty-one days**, Ryza's relic; 'I was on the right team when you were still learning to read.'
- **~1407 THE MAJOR, line 2:** 'Sure... I saw the stuff. So you're supposedly a Wolfer follower, then. I won't believe you're the high and mighty man you are... but you're blind, aren't you? Then tell me what's outside, Interrogator, since you're so sure.'
- **~1408 THE MAJOR, line 3:** 'Who is above you outside of Gallowmere? Tell me and I'll tell you the outcome of your so-called world. Persuasion (a bargain) NATURAL 20 + 3 - 1 = 22: the 20+ band (d2 2: 10%), DOUBLED: -20% -> 65%.** Unbroken, cracked. True, and to him safe (known to the Inquisition already): **'The Captain. Wolfer. - **~1411 THE MAJOR, line 5:** 'You mean you struck them because you're Wolfer's dog.' **Gets in (new: the page itself says 'by order of the Captain': his 'own hand' was obedience). The loaders you can already guess at. And a ship that's coming for grain that won't be there.'** (A ship is due for the Lamps grain; its name and master still held.)
- **~1414 THE MAJOR, line 7:** 'Holding it for what? It's over. Your fight, your team, Wolfer... and much more. Your inside man gave me a lot that helps my work here. - **~1416 THE MAJOR, line 9:** 'He fought, he did. Went down hard too, as well as the rest of your team. But too bad you went down first. And you're a true leader? - **~1420-1435 THE MAJOR, BROKEN: the follow-ups (the player: 'ask him any more questions I need to know'; set now, true, what he knows):**

  - **The Light: the 6th knew 'an Inquisition team with a white bird' took the Mart; no names, no faces.**

You know it's me.'), **Nessa** ('Little sister's coming whether you like it or not.'), **Corvane** ('I'll go.'), **Varga** ('Your back, boss.'). Asked of the player: the volunteer; then the fourth team's captain (four cells; Kell and Hollis captain two).
- **~1550 Tamsin's assignments:** **LOWMERE: Varga in charge** ('Lowmere seems to be an easy one, with just a night crew'); **SALTBEND: Kell, a team of four** ('the lighters will be a different task'); **KETTLE FORD: Tamsin's two-man squad** ('a whole family who might go sideways'); **VARN QUAY: Hollis.** (Varga: 'Lowmere. Mine.' Kell: 'Saltbend. Four. Done.' Hollis: 'Varn Quay, boss. The weighers won't know what weighed them.') **Numbers: 12 on the ground: Tamsin + 1, Kell + 3, Varga + 2, Hollis + 2.** (Hidden: Kettle Ford and Saltbend are kill-team jobs, 35 each; Lowmere and Varn Quay small, 10 each.) Tamsin's volunteer still to pick ('in a moment').
- **~1552 THE TEAMS (Tamsin's picks):** **KETTLE FORD: Tamsin and NESSA** ('we do this quick and quiet, alright? And try not to have me save you again.'). **SALTBEND: KELL, Dace, Ferro, Morcant.** **VARN QUAY: HOLLIS, Sorel, Tavi.** **LOWMERE: VARGA, Corvane, Isk.** Aboard: Brenner (astropath, vox), Sallow and Tey (Vosk), Mira (Valk) and Teo (ship) flying. (Nessa: 'Save me? I seem to remember a certain interrogator flat on his back in the dirt at Fourteen, big brother.' Dace, not with Tamsin for once: 'Brother.' A long look. 'Fine. Don't you dare get hurt without me.')
- **~1554 Brenner put the survey up on the briefing table (the ship's approach auspex laid over Varrow's Administratum charts). GALLOWMERE'S RIVER, SET NOW (consistent with the Gallowmere map: IX ~300 km upriver of Hearthgate; XIV ~140 km NW of IX):** the main river (**the Gallow**) runs down from the north-west plateau to **Hearthgate at the confluence** with its tributary (**the Varn**), then on through the salt marshes to the sea. **Along it, from the top: XIV on the plateau (0 km; the ship holding over it now) · KETTLE FORD (~70 km down: a ford and ferry village where the inland road crosses; the Dunns' punts) · IX (~140 km) · LOWMERE (~260 km: marsh lowlands; the Corn Exchange) · HEARTHGATE (~440 km: the confluence, the main grain port; Silo Row 3; the Governor's house in the upper town) · SALTBEND (~540 km: the salt marshes near the coast; the lightermen's guild hall).** **VARN QUAY: ~150 km up the Varn from Hearthgate (~400 km from XIV direct): the tithe-weighers' house.** **The Valk ~500 km/h: Kettle Ford ~10 min from here, Lowmere ~30, Hearthgate and Varn Quay ~50, Saltbend ~65.**
- **~1556 The drop plan (Tamsin: 'Kettle and Low drop first, then Hearth and Salt'; 'Hearth' = Hollis's Varn Quay team, via Hearthgate; ALL FOUR HIT TOGETHER at one H-hour once the last team is down):** **Mira's Valk: Tamsin and Nessa at Kettle Ford (~10 min), then Varga's team at Lowmere (~20 min on);** **the *Bright Light* (Teo) down the river: Hollis's team up the Varn at Varn Quay, then Kell's at Saltbend.** H-hour and the orders for the cells (alive or whatever's easier): asked of the player.
- **~1600 The orders for the cells (Tamsin): 'Leaders: try to have them alive. Anyone else... I don't want any more prisoners.'** (Garrow, Hesk, the Dunn father, Fenn alive if it can be done; the rest killed, surrender or not: the old standing order, cf. the tug dock.) **H-hour 1900 (dusk; full dark ~2030), all four together.** Kell: 'Leaders breathing, the rest don't. Understood.' A short silence round the table; Sorel looked at his hands; nobody argued. **Launch ~1700.**
- **~1605 Tamsin to Ollin: rekit his gear for tonight (routine), and any suppressor that fits Stinger?** **Ollin: rekitted by ~1630 (Vigil: a full hot-shot pack seated, the 20/30 to the charging rack, 1 spare full; Stinger: a full stick of 40 and 1 spare; Shameholder cleaned and the chain tensioned; carapace checked; the refractor field's cell topped).** **Suppressor for Stinger: no:** a Kasr Hornet's barrel takes no muzzle can (catalogue), and Dace's custom barrel was never threaded; threading it is a Dace job of a few hours (he's going out tonight); and **there's no loose can in stores** (none logged). **Ollin's offer instead: the silenced stub pistol from the Quay 4 haul** (built-in standard silencer, catalogue stub.md; cleaned by Ollin: Clean), with a box of rounds.
- **~1610 Tamsin's stealth kit from Ollin: the silenced stub pistol (Quay 4 haul; Clean; 8 in the box magazine, heavy subsonic lead slugs, + a box of 30), the MESH ARMOUR (reinforced-flak tier, silent; in place of his heavy carapace for tonight) and THE DARK RAGS (the infiltrator's), buckles and slings taped; his own combat knife (Vigil's bayonet).** Not taken: the mono-knife, smokes, the photon flash, the choke gas. (The refractor field and his long guns and Shameholder: asked of the player.)
- **~1612 Tamsin's carry for Kettle Ford (final): the silenced stub pistol (8 + 30), his combat knife, SHAMEHOLDER (just in case: loud), the mesh armour, the dark rags, the REFRACTOR FIELD (kept).** Vigil, Stinger, Last Word and the Kasr Frontier stay aboard. Nessa in her own infiltrator's kit.
- **~1615 Tamsin to Ollin: the good stuff in the hauls, and anything to speed up the schematics?** **Ollin's run-down (from the IX, Hearthgate and Fourteen hauls against Ferro's list and the wishlist):** **plasma cells off the 4 Ryza plasma pistols (2 IX, 2 Fourteen) and the wrecked Mars plasma gun: Kell's spare cell (wishlist), and stock Ferro can tune himself instead of waiting on the 2 tuned cells on order to the Rest (DM default: Ferro tunes one in a bench-day: schematic 15 needn't wait)**; **2 melta bombs: Tavi's wishlist**; **the dorm commander's map-slate: Hollis's wishlist** (once Brenner's read it); **2 Scion-pattern helmets** (rare: +2 Perception, +1 on shooting); **the Enginseer's repair kit (IX): DM default: while Dace or Ferro work with it, each build takes one bench-day less (min 1)**; **the intact Guard jump pack (IX): Isk's, no repair needed** (his old one waits on Ferro); **3 inferno pistols** (melta: armour and vehicles); **power weapons: the duelist's power sword, 3 commissars' power swords, 3 power knives**; **13 storm trooper carapace** (spares, or for sale); 2 bolt pistols; hot-shot packs; IX's 7 kraks; the long-range vox set (Brenner).
- **~1617 Issued (Tamsin): the intact Guard jump pack (IX) to ISK** (his old stolen one, still waiting on Ferro, now the spare); **a Scion-pattern helmet to KELL** (+2 Perception, +1 on shooting; replaces his helm with the pict lens: helmets don't stack). The second Scion helmet: Tamsin deciding.
- **~1620 Tamsin: Kell's pict lens moved off his old helm onto his Scion helmet (Ollin fits it: a few minutes' work); the second Scion helmet to ISK** (Ollin: his pict lens moved over the same way). **Ollin tells them when they come for their kits for tonight.** Issued tonight: Isk (Guard jump pack, Scion helmet), Kell (Scion helmet with his pict lens).
- **~1622 Tamsin asked Ollin for the best power sword aboard. The five, rolled for condition (d6: 1-2 worn, 3-4 sound, 5-6 fine; set now):** the duelist's stolen sword 1 (worn: its field flickers, the cell nearly spent); commissar trophy A (Fourteen) 1 (worn); **commissar trophy B (Fourteen) 5: FINE**; commissar trophy C (Fourteen) 1 (worn); commissar trophy D (the choir-house, Hearthgate) 4 (sound). **The best: JUNIOR COMMISSAR VARO's power sword (d3 3)**: a commissar's sabre with a gilded aquila basket hilt, the blade etched on the forte 'VARO · MOEBIAN VI · KALT'; the trooper who took it kept it clean as a trophy; field and cell sound. (Power sword: medium, chains 2; power weapons go through carapace, heavy carapace, ceramite and power armour.)
- **~1624 Varo's power sword: Tamsin's OFF WEAPON** ('for when I need something heavy-hitting, more than Shame... don't tell it I said that'); on the campaign sheet; kept aboard until called for (not carried tonight). (Ollin: 'My lips are sealed. That chainsword's got ears.')
- **~1626 Tamsin to Ollin: clean the sabre and get rid of the etching.** (Ollin, after a moment's look at the name: 'Aye, boss.' The etching ground off and the blade cleaned while the teams launch.) **Tamsin went to find Nessa:** she's in the bunk room, kitting up.
- **FIXED (~1628): Ollin can't have given Tamsin the mesh armour and the dark rags: both are NESSA's, worn since day 34 (campaign sheet: chest-only carapace over the mesh; the dark rags), and there's no second set aboard.** The DM's error, told to the player. **Tamsin's armour tonight falls back to the stealth kit: his own carapace (heavy front) taped, under a dark field cover, with the refractor field** (the player may change it). His carry otherwise as set: Shameholder, the silenced stub pistol (8 + 30), his combat knife.
- **~1628 Tamsin to Nessa: 'What are you bringing?'** Her kit (the sheet): **the needle pistol (10-dart magazine; black-tip 8, shard 10, panic 10, tracker 4 on hand)**, **the Sisters (6 Callidus throwing knives) and her own 2**, **her power knife**, **the toxin needle-blade**, chest-only carapace over the mesh, no helm, leathers and the dark rags, 2 smokes, a rebreather.
- **~1635-1708 Launch.** Tamsin and Nessa to the hangar ('Good. Now let's go. You ready?' / Nessa: 'Born ready, boss.'). **Mira's Valk lifted at 1700 with Tamsin, Nessa and Varga's team (Varga, Corvane, Isk); the *Bright Light* (Teo) went down the river with Kell's and Hollis's teams.** **~1708 KETTLE FORD from the Valk, high and wide at dusk (no roll; what anyone would see):** the Gallow wide and shallow over a gravel ford; the inland road coming down to it from the north on a causeway; **a ferry landing with a dozen flat grain punts tied up and a chain-ferry raft**; **not a family farm: a village: ~25 houses of grey stone along both banks, a ferry-house, a long punt shed, a chapel with a squat bell-tower, smoke from chimneys; people about: men on the landing, women at a pump, children by the water.** (Hidden: 35 of the garden there, steady commoners; a kill-team job.) **Where to drop: asked of the player.**
- **~1712 THE DROP: Tamsin: 'Put us down far away. Me and Nessa will come in on foot.'** **Mira's quiet drop (d20 + 6 stealth flying vs hidden 12: dusk, ~3 km out, behind trees): 6 + 6 = 12: UNHEARD.** Down in **a windbreak of old elms on the upper bank, ~3 km north-west of Kettle Ford**, off the inland road; the Valk lifted at once for Lowmere with Varga's team. **The ground to the village (set now):** standing tithe grain chest-high and gold (the Lamps harvest not yet in), cut by irrigation ditches and thorn hedgerows; **the inland road** on its raised causeway to the east, running straight into the village; **the Gallow** to the west, its bank thick with reeds and willows down to the ford. ~45 minutes' careful walk; they'd be in sight of the houses by ~1800 with an hour to look before H-hour.
- **~1715-1805 The river route (Tamsin: 'The river. Let's go.'). Approach event d20 16: quiet.** Fifty minutes along the Gallow under willows and through reeds, boots in the mud; **in the willows ~150 m upstream of the ferry landing on the near (north) bank by ~1805**, the light going. **In plain sight (no roll):** the landing with its punts and the chain-ferry raft; **6-8 men stacking grain sacks from two punts into the long punt shed**; the ferry-house; the near bank's dozen houses and the chapel with its squat bell-tower; the far bank's dozen houses across the ford; lamps being lit in windows; a woman calling children in; woodsmoke and baking bread. **Tamsin's Perception (d20 + 9), hidden numbers set now:** **10:** the men on the landing are armed (stub guns and an autogun slung, a few cleavers); **13:** the Dunns' house: the biggest, by the ferry-house, a boatwright's sign carved DUNN over the door, the old man on its step watching the landing; **15:** **a lookout up in the chapel bell-tower, with a lamp and a horn** (the village's alarm); **18:** the scale: **this is the cell, the whole village of it**: more men coming and going from the punt shed with guns than any family; a sentry on the causeway bridge too.
- **~1806 Tamsin's Perception: 15 + 9 = 24: beats 10, 13, 15 and 18: ALL of it seen** (the armed men on the landing; the Dunns' house; the bell-tower lookout with a lamp and a horn; the scale: the whole village is the cell, more armed men in and out of the punt shed, a sentry on the causeway bridge). Tamsin (said before he'd taken it in): 'A pretty village that won't know what hit them. Let's move in house by house. Let's start with the closest one.' **The DM flagged it: H-hour is 1900 (all four cells together); moving now means if it goes loud, Kettle Ford fights alone for 55 minutes and a cell with a vox could warn the others. Asked: go now, or wait for 1900.**
- **~1806-1812 Tamsin: go now, quiet ('fudge it'). The first house (upstream end): Stealth: Tamsin 11 + 10 = 21, Nessa 9 + 7 = 16, vs the watchers 9 + 1 = 10: UNSEEN. Occupants (d6 1): EMPTY** (its man at the landing; supper half laid, a cleaning rag and a half-empty box of stub rounds). **The second house (20 m on; lamp lit, voices): Stealth: Nessa 12 + 7 = 19 vs the watchers 6 + 1 = 7: unseen; Tamsin can't fail (+10 vs 7): unseen.** **Occupants (d6 6): TWO MEN OF THE CELL cleaning guns at the kitchen table** (an autogun stripped on a cloth, a stub revolver), a lamp between them. **Overheard through the back window (set now):** the smoke on the plateau this morning ('the high field's burning'), IX silent since last night, old Dunn uneasy; **the village's alarm: if the bell rings, every man to the punt shed (their armoury and strongpoint) and the women and children into the chapel crypt.**
- **~1815 The side window: Tamsin lifted the shutter's latch with his knife (DEX 7 + 10 = 17 vs hidden 10: silent) and climbed into the dark back room; Nessa followed (Stealth 3 + 7 = 10 vs the men's hearing 3 + 1 - 2 busy = 2: unheard).** **The back room (d6 5 on 1-3 a bedroom, 4-5 a sleeping child, 6 a gun rack): A SLEEPING CHILD:** a boy of about five in a box-bed under a patched quilt, a carved wooden punt on the sill. The inner door to the kitchen stands ajar: lamplight, the two men's voices, the smell of gun oil.
- **~1817 Into the kitchen: Tamsin and Nessa stepped through behind the two men, backs to the wall beside the inner door, and eased the boy's door shut (Nessa's Stealth 6 + 7 = 13; the men's notice 2 + 1 - 2 = 1: nothing; Tamsin can't fail, +10).** Unnoticed. **The kitchen:** the table in the middle under the lamp; **the revolver man with his back to them (~2 m), thumbing rounds in: four of six loaded**; **the autogun man side-on (~3 m), the gun's bolt in his hands, the rest in pieces on the cloth**; the hearth on the left wall; the front door to the lane on the far wall; the back window. Tamsin's next move: asked.
- **~1818 THE KITCHEN (Kettle Ford's first fight; boss meter d100 75 vs 1%: no boss; surprise: the two men can't act or defend).** Tamsin signalled Nessa to the near man, counted on his fingers, one... two..., then stepped out: **the silenced pistol at the autogun man's head (3 m: 7 + 1 for the small target = 8; d20 + 8 DEX + 4 prof + 2 surprise = +14; the player's 9, 20, 9): 23: HIT (Hurt); NATURAL 20, Tamsin's crit (19-20): 36: two steps: THE AUTOGUN MAN DOWN; the leftover 9 → 24 into the revolver man as Nessa's blade went in.** **Nessa (the needle-blade, +5 DEX + 2 surprise; he can't defend): 22, 22, 16: three strikes: THE REVOLVER MAN DOWN.** Both finished on the order (no prisoners): Nessa, quick and silent. **Noise: anyone outside (d20 + 1 vs 15): 9 + 1 = 10: nobody.** **THE BOY WOKE (d6 2):** from behind the shut door, a small voice: 'Da?' (the revolver man was his father; set now).
